# Codex verification brief — relation map on the try-it page (commit bfcb83d)

You are an independent reviewer. Do NOT edit any tracked file. Write scratch files only under
`audit_evidence_claims/m1/`. Put your report in `audit_evidence_claims/m1/REPORT.md`.
End with exactly one line: `VERDICT: APPROVE` or `VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED`.

## What was added

`src/app.py` `Engine.explore(text, per_group=30)` and `POST /api/explore`; `web/try.html` section "0. 관계 지도"
(`drawMap`, `explore`, `itemDetail`, the GKO/TKO/ACT/PH tables, click-through and the "따라온 길" trail);
`tests/test_app.py::test_explore_draws_a_drug_and_refuses_a_biosimilar`. Run tests with
`python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider` (317). Start the page with `python src/app.py [port]` (use a port
other than 8765, which may already be in use) and exercise it over HTTP.

The page is for a domain reviewer to look at one node (drug, target, company, investigator, trial, disease) and
everything a live edge ties it to, grouped by relation, and to click through. It is read-only.

## The guarantees claimed (page text in section 0 and the `explore` docstring)

1. Every neighbour shown is joined to the centre by a live (not retired) edge to a live node, and the group label
   states the relation and its direction correctly (e.g. "이 약의 임상을 주관한 곳 (주관 ≠ 개발)" for
   Company -SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF-> Drug seen from the drug).
2. Details shown for a neighbour are the stored values: trial phases/status/start/title/url, sponsor trial count
   (`evidence_trials`) and sponsor class, drug trial count in the graph (EVALUATES), max phase, action type.
3. A name the question linker refuses (biosimilar, distinct product, tracer) is reported as refused, never drawn
   as the originator molecule; an unknown name says "not found".
4. Groups are ordered by size and each list keeps the `per_group` neighbours with most evidence; the page says
   when a list is cut and shows the true total.
5. Nothing is written; no model is called.

## What to verify (reproduce; cite file:line and exact inputs)

A. **Wrong relations or directions.** Any centre where an edge is grouped under the wrong label or direction,
   self-loops (src == dst), edges with both ends equal to the centre, PARENT_OF/SPONSORS/STUDIES/ASSOCIATED_WITH
   direction wording, Investigator vs Company handling, nodes whose type the tables do not cover.
B. **Name resolution.** `explore` takes the first non-refused hit of `link_entities`. Inputs with several entities
   ("osimertinib EGFR"), a refused token next to a real one ("Mvasi bevacizumab"), salts vs parents, company aliases
   with `also_matching_ids` (only the first id is drawn — is that misleading?), node ids vs names that collide,
   case/whitespace/Unicode, long inputs. Is anything drawn that the input does not name, or a refused product drawn
   as the originator?
C. **Wrong values.** Trial counts, sponsor counts, max phase and action shown versus the database
   (`data/processed/oncokg.sqlite`); retired edges/nodes leaking in; counts computed over retired edges;
   the `trials` count chunking; JSON props that are null or malformed.
D. **Cutting and ordering.** Is the "most evidence" ordering what the page says (trials are ordered by start date
   string — is that what the text implies)? Does the map's subset (`drawMap` allocation) ever hide a whole group
   or misstate totals? The Disease node (thousands of neighbours) — performance and output size.
E. **Page safety and correctness.** HTML/SVG injection from labels, titles, ids, URLs (`href` with `javascript:`?),
   edge ids in `data-id` attributes, the trail/back buttons after errors, double clicks, refused/not-found states,
   dark mode legibility is not in scope.
F. **Server.** Error handling for empty/oversized input, concurrency with the lock, behaviour when the DB has
   retired rows.
G. **Docs and wording.** Section 0 text, README/README.ko lines for `app.py`, the module docstring: do they claim
   more than the code does? Korean labels in GKO/ACT/PH that misstate the stored code.

## Approval bar

No known way for the map to show a relation that is not a live edge, a wrong direction or relation wording, a
value that differs from the stored one, a refused product drawn as the originator, or script injection. Report
should-fix items (misleading wording, cut lists without notice) separately from blockers.

---
name: review
description: Review the changes since a fixed point (commit, branch, tag, or merge-base) along two axes ??Standards (does the code follow this repo's documented coding standards?) and Spec (does the code match what the originating issue/PRD asked for?). Runs both reviews in parallel sub-agents and reports them side by side. Use when the user wants to review a branch, a PR, work-in-progress changes, or asks to "review since X".
---

Two-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:

- **Standards** ??does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?
- **Spec** ??does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / PRD / spec?

Both axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.

The issue tracker should have been provided to you ??run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` if `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing.

## Process

### 1. Pin the fixed point

Whatever the user said is the fixed point ??a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc. If they didn't specify one, ask for it.

Capture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.

Before going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here ??not inside two parallel sub-agents.

### 2. Identify the spec source

Look for the originating spec, in this order:

1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.) ??fetch via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.
2. A path the user passed as an argument.
3. A PRD/spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.
4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report "no spec available".

### 3. Identify the standards sources

Anything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.

### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel

Send a single message with two `Agent` tool calls. Use the `general-purpose` subagent for both.

**Standards sub-agent prompt** ??include:

- The full diff command and commit list.
- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3.
- The brief: "Report ??per file/hunk where relevant ??every place the diff violates a documented standard. Cite the standard (file + the rule). Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words."

**Spec sub-agent prompt** ??include:

- The diff command and commit list.
- The path or fetched contents of the spec.
- The brief: "Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words."

If the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.

### 5. Aggregate

Present the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings ??the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).

End with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes ??that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.

## Why two axes

A change can pass one axis and fail the other:

- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing ??**Standards pass, Spec fail.**
- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions ??**Spec pass, Standards fail.**

Reporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.

# Relation map — Codex review 1 (2026-11-03 KST)

Report: `audit_evidence_claims/m1/REPORT.md`. Verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED. Every finding reproduced before fixing.

| # | Finding | Repair |
| --- | --- | --- |
| B1 | "bevacizumab - awwb", "[89Zr] trastuzumab" drew the originator (linker joined tokens without the refusal checks) | `link_entities` refuses a multi-token window that names a distinct product or tracer, and none of its tokens link (fixes questions too) |
| B2 | a collaborator was labelled the trial's lead sponsor | SPONSORS groups keep `role`; headings say lead sponsor or collaborator |
| B3 | an investigator was labelled an institution | heading uses 연구자 for Investigator groups |
| B4 | a retired node could be explored by name | the centre must be live whatever path resolved it |
| count | drug trial counts included retired trials | counts join live Trial nodes |
| should-fix | ordering wording, ring cut silently, stale trail after errors, out-of-order replies, ambiguous names silent, action/class labels, omitted details, malformed props, `javascript:` URLs, "All three" | each group states its actual sort; ring says drawn/total; trail cleared on refusal/not-found/error; replies older than the latest request are dropped; the reading and other matches (company, target, refused span) are shown; stored action codes shown with a Korean gloss; sponsor class code shown; centre start date, drug count and phase always shown; props that are not objects read as empty; only clinicaltrials.gov links are clickable; docstring fixed |

Tests 321.

Round 2 (audit_evidence_claims/m2) stopped at Codex's usage limit before a report (log.txt ends with the limit error).
Its partial fixtures showed a SPONSORS edge without a role would be labelled collaborator; the page now says
"역할 기록 없음" for any role other than lead_sponsor/collaborator. Groups shown as "이름 순" were sorted Z to A; now A to Z
within equal weight. Round 2 to be re-run when the limit resets (2026-11-04 15:37).

## Round 2 re-run (audit_evidence_claims/m2b) — CHANGES REQUIRED

All m1 reproductions FIXED. New:

| # | Finding | Repair |
| --- | --- | --- |
| R1 | "osimertinib and generic version of bevacizumab": the refusal window swallowed osimertinib and left bevacizumab to link (map and QA switched subject) | a refused product span never crosses a comma or a joining word (and, or, plus, with, vs, …); separately named drugs survive in either order |
| R2 | malformed sponsor props displayed as "0건" | an unrecorded count is `evidence_trials: null`, shown as "기록 없음"; 0 is only a sort weight |
| should-fix | bool scores accepted and crashing render; rounded score; embedded multi-id alias alternatives dropped; "everything" docstring; silent 500-char cut | numbers exclude bool/NaN; score shown as stored; the hit's `also_matching_ids` disclosed; docstring; >500 chars is a 400 error; render errors shown |

Tests 323.

## Round 3 (audit_evidence_claims/m3) — CHANGES REQUIRED

| # | Finding | Repair |
| --- | --- | --- |
| M3-B1 | "with" as a joiner let "paclitaxel with albumin bound nanoparticles" draw PACLITAXEL (and QA answer with it) | refusal is now bound to the drug it qualifies: the shortest span holding that drug and no other drug, inside one list item; "with" is no longer a joiner |
| M3-B2 | "What is the generic version of bevacizumab?" drew BEVACIZUMAB (inherited) | same repair: the span must contain the drug, so the question prefix no longer absorbs the qualifier |
| should-fix | the single-token qualifier pass crossed commas ("osimertinib, liposomal doxorubicin" refused osimertinib) | neighbours are taken only within the same list item and never a joining word |
| found while fixing | words of a longer name were read again ("erlotinib hydrochloride" also listed ERLOTINIB) | a matched span is not re-linked |

Tests 325.

## Round 4 (audit_evidence_claims/m4) — CHANGES REQUIRED

Shortest-span binding kept failing (wrong drug on ties, a six-token reach, qualifiers already consumed by the token pass).
Replaced by a fail-closed rule: text is cut into list items at , ; ， ； 、 and listing words; an item in which any run of
words names a distinct product or tracer has every drug in it refused (which drug the qualifier describes is not guessed);
drugs in other items, and targets/companies in the same item, still link. M4-B1 (both drugs now refused, never
BEVACIZUMAB), M4-B2 (no distance cap), M4-B3 (one pass, no pre-consumed qualifier) and the EGFR/AstraZeneca over-refusal
are covered by tests. Tests 326.

## Round 5 (audit_evidence_claims/m5) — CHANGES REQUIRED

| # | Finding | Repair |
| --- | --- | --- |
| M5-B1 | a glued "/" or "|" ("bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib") hid the suffix guard; the alias key dropped the slash and linked BEVACIZUMAB | tokens are stripped of "/" and "|", which end the list item |
| M5-B2 | "liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin": "and" detached the bare qualifier | a qualifier item with no drug carries across a listing word to the next item's drugs; a product named by itself (in the index, or a known biosimilar/reformulation brand such as Mvasi, Abraxane) does not carry |
| M5-S1 | names were built across list items ("biosimilar anastrozole, Arimidex" lost Arimidex; "Erlotinib, hydrochloride ..." became the salt) | drug spans and the positive matcher stay inside one item |
| M5-S3 | the all-runs scan was cubic (1.8 s at 250 words, under the lock) | whole item once for unanchored patterns, runs of at most eight words for anchored ones: 500 words in about 0.03 s |

# Independent relation-map review — round 10

Reviewed **`c364c84a289cfc3da5981708db3547ff6c752bde`**, 2026-11-03 KST. **Changes required:** every previously reported originator-blocking reproduction is now fixed, but four independently reproduced name-resolution defect families remain: **two regressions introduced by the latest query change and two inherited holes**. A map can contain entirely genuine graph edges and still be wrong because it selected a molecule the qualified input does not name.

No tracked file was edited. All new scripts, fixtures, logs and this report are under `audit_evidence_claims/m10/`. The explicitly accepted M5-S2, two hydroxyethyl rapamycin forms and `Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate` over-refusals are **not blockers**.

## Verification and evidence limits

- **337 passed in 28.38 s**, exit 0, using `python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider`, bytecode disabled and temporary files under m10. [Output](root_checks/pytest.txt), [environment and hashes](root_checks/pytest_summary.json).
- Reused the six historical root HTTP runners, four M6 HTTP runners, M8's 57-request confirmation, and M9's 54-request independent confirmation; all exit 0. Port-sharing runners ran sequentially. [Manifest](root_checks/reuse_manifest.json), [six runners](root_checks/execution_http.json), [M6](root_checks/execution_m6_http.json), [M8/M9](root_checks/execution_extra.json).
- Historical naming: **37 leaf scripts**, including all five exact M1 scripts, the **113-case / 7,511-key** sweep, M7's **93 inputs / 10 QA / 44 HTTP** cases, M8's **174 inputs / 8 QA** including all **26 Unicode Pd** characters, and M9's **146 inputs / 16 QA / 50 HTTP** cases. The old alias sweep finishes without cache errors. [Name report](names/FINDINGS.md), [reuse manifest](names/reuse_manifest.json), [50 name-related dispositions](names/historical_name_dispositions.json).
- The frozen **1,648-question** no-relink counterfactual has **64 identical / 1,560 entity-metadata-only / 24 semantic differences / 0 errors**. Its inherited adaptation patches current `_link`; obsolete old monkey-patches are not used as current behavioral evidence. [Result](names/adapted_frozen_qa_relink_sweep.json).
- Independent graph oracle: **6,469 live centres, 22 relation/direction/type/role combinations, zero errors**. All **19 DB assertions** and **24 UI assertions** pass, including live/retired counts, missing/malformed props, exact score values, 1,001-drug chunking, ordering, cut notices, escaping, trail clearing and stale-response suppression. [DB/UI report](db_ui/FINDINGS.md), [oracle](db_ui/db_validate.json), [DB](db_ui/db_assertions.json), [UI](db_ui/ui_assertions.json).
- All **36 page demos plus six map callbacks** pass; all **36 complete demo responses equal m7, m8 and m9**. All 36 original-CLI HTTP demo requests are 200 and equal direct Engine responses. The seven original QA demo evidence counts remain **12, 12, 11, 1, 12, 0, 0**; six full responses match original `bfcb83d`, with only Mvasi refusal metadata changed. [Demos](db_ui/demo_previous_comparison.json), [HTTP](db_ui/demo_http.json), [original comparison](root_checks/http_demo_results.json).
- Fresh attack sweep: **1,224 inputs**, recording raw resolver, resolver veto, item linker, whole-name shortcut, public linker and map. **35 focused inputs** compare exact parent `3a362eb` and current query modules against the same current app/rules/database. These are query-module counterfactuals, not a reconstructed old deployment. Root separately confirms **95 actual HTTP responses** using the original `src/app.py` CLI. [Attack report](attacks/FINDINGS.md), [sweep](attacks/probe_results.json), [parent comparison](attacks/focus_results.json), [57 HTTP responses](root_checks/candidate_http.json), [38 HTTP responses](root_checks/isotope_http.json).
- UI tests execute actual page JavaScript in a Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. They establish generated markup and callback/state behavior, not browser paint or browser-executed XSS. Original-CLI HTTP served bytes match `web/try.html`. No server used port 8765. Findings below use the repository's own isotope/product predicates, dictionary identities and controlled spellings; they do not require external clinical assumptions.

## Blocking findings

### M10-B1 — P1, new regression: a stored alias absorbs its trailing isotope

Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"A33 124i"}`.

**Actual:** HTTP 200, **ADAKITUG / CHEMBL5314551**, no refusal. Its map draws the stored **ACTS_ON → CXCL8 (ENSG00000169429), INHIBITOR** edge. The centre has **0 trials**; trial and sponsor QA both have zero evidence, so this is not a claim of invented trial rows. The dictionary records `A33 124i` as an alias of the unlabelled ADAKITUG antibody. The repository identifies `124i` as an isotope (`src/textrules.py:289-298`). Exact parent query code refused this input. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/candidate_http.json), [dictionary, stages and predecessor](attacks/focus_results.json).

| Current stage | `A33 124i` result |
|---|---|
| Raw resolver | ALIAS → CHEMBL5314551 |
| Resolver veto | `{}` |
| Item/public linker | Drug ADAKITUG, no Refused |
| Whole-name shortcut | Drug ADAKITUG |
| Map | ADAKITUG with its CXCL8 relation |

**Cause:** `_marked` computes `own = ["A33", "124i"]`, but checks only `own[:1]` and `own[:2]`, not the trailing isotope (`src/query.py:248-252`). The accepted match already consumed `124i`, so it is no longer in the adjacent `nxt` words. The whole-name shortcut receives no veto and accepts the same stored alias (`258-275`). This recreates the stored tracer-alias risk explicitly discussed in the round-9 repair note without adding an explicit exemption.

Controls/variants: `A33 124 i`, `A33 [124i]`, and `A33 124-I` also select ADAKITUG; preferred name `ADAKITUG` correctly remains available. `124i A33` is **not_found**, not a successful refusal control. Do not generalize this finding to every restored isotope-bearing stored name: several other names identify an explicitly labelled drug node themselves.

**Required outcome:** an isotope at the end of an accepted stored alias must not disappear from the refusal decision merely because the positive matcher consumed it.

### M10-B2 — P1, new regression: an isotope token is substituted for the named substance

Exact input **`18F fluorothymidine`** returns HTTP 200, **FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18 / CHEMBL2030434 / 1 trial**, no refusal. Trial QA returns its **NCT00923273** evidence row; sponsor QA has one row. **`18F nonexistingdrug`** gives the same map and evidence, proving that recognizing the isotope token alone is sufficient to invent a positive identity for an unknown labelled substance. `18-F fluorothymidine` and `[18F] fluorothymidine` also reproduce. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/isotope_http.json), [full QA/stages](attacks/focus_results.json).

For the unbracketed inputs every refusal stage misses: raw resolver **UNRESOLVED**, veto `{}`, item/public linker matches only **`18F`**, whole-name `None`. The dictionary has a standalone `18f` synonym for fludeoxyglucose; the remainder does not name that molecule. Exact parent query code refused the longer inputs. Both versions permit the lone `18f` alias control, so preserving valid lone-name behavior does not require this regression.

**Cause:** `_marked` excludes any isotope test equal to the matched name itself via **`ws != own`** (`src/query.py:252`) without requiring that token to be alone in its item. The positive matcher then emits it as a Drug (`384-402`). The protection intended for lone isotope-shaped names is thus applied inside a longer product description. Bare `fluorothymidine` is not_found, but prefixing an isotope produces an unrelated positive map.

**Required outcome:** keep supported lone aliases while preventing an isotope word from replacing the unknown or different substance it labels. This is a wrong selected identity, not a fabricated graph edge.

### M10-B3 — P1, inherited hole: punctuation consumes the two-word isotope budget

Exact inputs **`trastuzumab 89 - Zr`** and **`trastuzumab [89 - Zr]`** return HTTP 200, **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585 / 13 trials**, no refusal. Trial QA returns **12** originator rows; direct sponsor QA also returns **12**. `trastuzumab 89 - Zr, osimertinib` still selects TRASTUZUMAB. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/candidate_http.json), [complete stages/QA](attacks/focus_results.json).

Raw resolver accepts the unlabelled drug; resolver veto is `{}`; item/public linker emits Drug without Refused; whole-name is `None`. Controls **`trastuzumab 89 Zr`**, **`trastuzumab 89-Zr`**, and **`trastuzumab [89.Zr]`** refuse. Current prefix **`89 - Zr trastuzumab`** also refuses. These are single-item inputs, with no comma/slash/list-word interpretation needed.

**Cause:** `after` takes only two original whitespace tokens at **`src/query.py:245`**: `89` and `-`. `_alnum_words` removes `-` only afterward (`250`), so `Zr` never reaches the isotope predicate. The promised limit is one or two **alnum words, any separator**, not two raw tokens. The underscore-separator variant also leaks. Parent query code leaks too; this is newly reported inherited incompleteness, not a claim that M9's exact repaired examples regressed.

**Required outcome:** apply the adjacency budget to the normalized alnum words rather than letting punctuation-only tokens exhaust it.

### M10-B4 — P1, inherited hole: normalized multiword product qualifiers still miss every guard

| Exact input | HTTP map | Trial QA evidence | Refusing control |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `generic-version of bevacizumab` | BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 / 122 trials | 12 | `generic version of bevacizumab` |
| `albumin - bound paclitaxel` | PACLITAXEL / CHEMBL428647 / 234 trials | 12 | `albumin-bound paclitaxel` |

Both are HTTP 200 without refusal. Sponsor QA also has 12 rows for each. `generic_version of bevacizumab` and `follow-on-biologic bevacizumab` are additional direct reproductions. Raw resolver, resolver veto and item linker all miss; whole-name supplies no veto. Exact parent query code leaks too. [Independent HTTP and controls](root_checks/candidate_http.json), [stages and parent comparison](attacks/focus_results.json).

**Cause:** `_qualified` retains whitespace or folds punctuation to hyphen (`src/query.py:125-138`), but the underlying patterns require whitespace in `generic\s+version` and `on\s+biologic`, or at most one whitespace/hyphen in `albumin[\s\-]?bound` (`src/textrules.py:142-149`). Thus underscore folding still produces an unrecognized `generic-version`, and spaces around a hyphen defeat `albumin-bound`. The words qualify the same product within one item; this is separate from the accepted list-boundary policy.

**Required outcome:** equivalent separator forms of an explicit product qualifier must preserve refusal before its originator is accepted. Bare molecule and separately listed valid-drug controls must remain distinguishable.

|---|---|

## Spec axis

**Four blocking defect families:** M10-B1/B2 are new query regressions; M10-B3/B4 are inherited holes. The original name-resolution guarantee and this round's adjacent-alnum isotope design are not met for the exact inputs above. ## Integrity and reproduction

The snapshot has **7 retired nodes, 142 retired edges, zero self-loops and zero live edges with retired endpoints**. `Engine.ask` uses `use_llm=False` (`src/app.py:180-181`); no model was called. Fixtures mutate in-memory DB copies. """Grounded question answering over the graph.

The retrieval step is the same resolver the pipeline uses, pointed at the
question instead of at a registry record. A question that names "Tagrisso" and a
graph that stores CHEMBL3353410 only meet if one vocabulary serves both, so the
dictionary is reused rather than reimplemented.

Three properties the answering layer holds to:

* **Every statement is traceable.** Each claim carries the edges it rests on,
  each edge carries its source record and fetch time.
* **The graph answers, not the model.** With a key, the language model is given
  the retrieved evidence and returns claims, not prose; each claim is checked
  against the graph and the answer is written from the accepted ones in the
  graph's own words (structured.py). With no key the evidence is rendered
  directly.
* **Nothing unsupported is shipped.** A claim the graph does not support is
  reported and left out. An unverifiable answer is worse than a short one.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import sys
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import config as C
import normalize as N
import textrules as T

DB_PATH = C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite"
TRIAL_URL = "https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/{}"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# evidence
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass
class Evidence:
    statement: str
    edge_id: str
    relation: str
    source: str
    source_record: str | None
    fetched_at: str | None
    confidence: float
    tier: str | None = None
    url: str | None = None
    detail: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def citation(self) -> str:
        base = self.source_record or self.source
        return f"[{base}]"


@dataclass
class Answer:
    question: str
    intent: str
    entities: list[dict]
    text: str
    evidence: list[Evidence]
    generated_by: str
    checks: dict

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "question": self.question,
            "intent": self.intent,
            "entities": self.entities,
            "answer": self.text,
            "generated_by": self.generated_by,
            "checks": self.checks,
            "evidence": [
                {"statement": e.statement, "relation": e.relation, "edge_id": e.edge_id,
                 "source": e.source, "source_record": e.source_record,
                 "fetched_at": e.fetched_at, "confidence": e.confidence,
                 "resolution_tier": e.tier, "url": e.url, **e.detail}
                for e in self.evidence
            ],
        }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# engine
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Words that list separately named things; a list item ends at one. "with" is not among
# them, because it also sits inside product descriptions ("paclitaxel with albumin bound
# nanoparticles").
_JOINERS = {"and", "or", "plus", "versus", "vs", "vs.", "+", "&", "then", "followed",
            "or/and", "and/or"}



# every Unicode dash (category Pd) and minus sign, read as a hyphen
_DASHES = "[" + "".join(re.escape(chr(c)) for c in range(0x110000)
                        if unicodedata.category(chr(c)) == "Pd" or chr(c) in "\u2212\u207b\u208b") + "]"


def _plain(text: str) -> str:
    """Accents off, as the alias key does."""
    t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "")
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFC", "".join(c for c in t if not unicodedata.combining(c)))


def _alnum_words(text: str) -> list[str]:
    """Letters-and-digits words, with every other character a separator."""
    return re.findall(r"[A-Za-z0-9]+", _plain(text))


def _fold(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(_DASHES, "-", unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text or ""))

def _qualified(words: list[str]) -> bool:
    """Does any part of this list item name a distinct product or a tracer?

    The unanchored patterns are tried on the whole item once; the anchored ones (a
    biosimilar suffix, a leading isotope) on every run of up to eight words, so the
    work grows linearly with the item."""
    def hit(text: str) -> bool:
        # the alias key ignores punctuation, underscores and accents, so the guards also
        # see the text with those folded ("bevacizumab-(awwb)", "bevacizumab_awwb",
        # "bevácizumab-awwb")
        plain = _plain(text)
        # and with every separator (punctuation, underscore, spaces) read as one space,
        # so "generic-version of", "albumin - bound" meet the multiword patterns
        return any(T.is_distinct_product(v) or T.is_radiotracer(v)
                   for v in {text, plain, re.sub(r"(?:[^\w\s]|_)+", "-", plain).strip("-"),
                             " ".join(_alnum_words(plain))})

    if hit(" ".join(words)):
        return True
    if any(T.canonical_key(w) in T.BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | T.REFORMULATION_NAMES for w in words):
        return True
    return any(hit(" ".join(words[i:j])) for i in range(len(words))
               for j in range(i + 1, min(len(words), i + 8) + 1))

class OncoKG:
    def __init__(self, db_path: Path = DB_PATH,
                 resolved_path: Path = C.PROCESSED / "resolved.json"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        resolved = json.loads(resolved_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.dic = resolved["dictionary"]
        self.index_keys = list(self.dic["index"].keys())
        self._resolve_cache: dict = {}
        self.disease = resolved["disease"]
        self.targets = {t["symbol"].lower(): t for t in resolved["targets"]
                        if t.get("symbol")}
        # one name can denote several organisations (a group and a subsidiary
        # renamed after it), so a key maps to every matching node, not one.
        self.companies: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        for row in self.conn.execute(
                "SELECT id,label FROM node WHERE type='Company' AND retired_at IS NULL"):
            self.companies.setdefault(T.canonical_key(row["label"]), []).append(row["id"])
            self.companies.setdefault(row["id"].removeprefix("ORG:"), []).append(row["id"])
        # a former name ("BeiGene") must still find the merged node
        for old, same in N.CURATED_SAME_ENTITY.items():
            if same in self.companies:
                self.companies.setdefault(old, []).extend(self.companies[same])
        self.companies = {k: sorted(set(v)) for k, v in self.companies.items()}

    # -- entity linking ---------------------------------------------------- #
    def link_entities(self, question: str) -> list[dict]:
        # one spelling for every check: compatibility forms folded, every dash a hyphen
        question = _fold(question)
        blocked = self._resolver_blocked(question)
        found = self._link(question)
        whole = self._whole_name(question, blocked, found)
        if whole:
            return [whole]
        if not blocked:
            return found
        # a drug the resolver refuses in this text is refused here too, whatever the
        # splitting below made of it (the two must agree before anything is linked)
        out = [e for e in found if not (e["type"] == "Drug" and e["id"] in blocked)]
        for text in sorted({t for t in blocked.values()}):
            if not any(e["type"] == "Refused" and (text in e["label"] or e["label"] in text)
                       for e in out):
                out.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": text, "matched_text": text,
                            "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not the "
                                   "originator molecule"})
        return out

    def _resolver_blocked(self, text: str) -> dict:
        """Drug ids inside the pieces the intervention resolver refuses as a distinct
        product or a tracer, with the refused piece."""
        comps = []
        lone = set()
        pieces = {p.strip() for p in re.split(r"[,;\uff0c\uff1b\u3001/|]", text) if p.strip()}
        for piece in pieces:                         # each distinct list piece once
            words = [w for w in re.split(r"[^\w\-]+", piece) if w]
            if not self._drug_spans(words, set(), [0] * len(words)):
                continue                             # no drug in it: nothing to refuse
            got = self._resolve_cache.get(piece)
            if got is None:
                try:
                    got = N.resolve_drug_string(piece, self.dic, self.index_keys)["components"]
                except Exception:                    # never let a guard crash a lookup
                    got = []
                if len(self._resolve_cache) >= 4096:
                    self._resolve_cache.clear()
                self._resolve_cache[piece] = got
            if len(words) == 1 and T.canonical_key(piece) in self.dic["index"]:
                lone.add(piece)                      # one stored name on its own ("566C")
            comps += got
        out: dict = {}
        accepted = {c.get("drug_id") for c in comps
                    if c.get("drug_id") and not str(c.get("tier", "")).startswith("BLOCKED")}
        for c in comps:
            piece = c.get("component") or ""
            tracer = "radiotracer_guard" in (c.get("rules") or []) or T.is_radiotracer(piece)
            if not (c.get("tier") == "BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT"
                    or (str(c.get("tier", "")).startswith("BLOCKED") and tracer)):
                continue
            if piece in lone:
                continue
            words = [w for w in re.split(r"[\s/|,;]+", piece) if w]
            words = [w.strip(" ?.!:()[]") or w for w in words]
            for a, b in self._drug_spans(words, set(), [0] * len(words)):
                key = T.canonical_key(" ".join(words[a:b]))
                for h in self.dic["index"].get(key, []):
                    # the same molecule named separately and accepted ("biosimilar
                    # anastrozole, Arimidex") is left to the list-item rule below
                    if h["drug_id"] not in accepted:
                        out[h["drug_id"]] = piece
        return out

    def _marked(self, key: str, did: str, tokens, seg, a: int, b: int):
        """The text that makes this drug name a distinct product or a tracer, if any.

        Checked on the same letters-and-digits form the name was looked up by: a known
        biosimilar suffix at the end of the name ("bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab_awwb")
        or as the next word, or an isotope label just before or after it ("[89.Zr]
        trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab")."""
        for sfx in T.BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES:
            stem = key[:-len(sfx)]
            if key.endswith(sfx) and stem and any(
                    h["drug_id"] == did for h in self.dic["index"].get(stem, [])):
                return " ".join(tokens[a:b])
        same = [k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] == seg[a]]
        before = [k for k in same if k < a]
        after = [k for k in same if k >= b]
        # words counted after normalisation, so punctuation never uses up the budget
        prev = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in before))[-2:]
        nxt = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in after))[:2]
        own = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[a:b]))
        lone = not before and not after      # the name is the whole list item
        # an isotope label anywhere in the name ("A33 124i"), or just before or after it
        # ("[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab 89 - Zr"); an isotope-shaped name counts
        # as a name only when it stands alone ("566C", "18f"), never as the substance
        # of a longer phrase ("18F fluorothymidine")
        inside = [own[i:j] for i in range(len(own)) for j in (i + 1, i + 2) if j <= len(own)]
        if lone and T.is_isotope_label(" ".join(own)):
            inside = []                      # the whole item is one isotope-shaped name
        name = " ".join(tokens[a:b])
        if nxt and nxt[0].lower() in T.BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES:
            return f"{name} {nxt[0]}"
        for ws in inside + [prev[-1:], prev, nxt[:1], nxt]:
            if ws and T.is_isotope_label(" ".join(ws)):
                return name if ws in inside else f"{name} ({' '.join(ws)})"
        return None

    def _whole_name(self, text: str, blocked: dict, found: list):
        """The whole input is exactly one stored drug name, letter for letter ("CP-358,774",
        "(E/Z)-AFATINIB"): read it whole rather than as the list its punctuation suggests.

        Only after every refusal has had its say: nothing the resolver or the list
        linker refused, and no product or tracer pattern in the name."""
        if blocked or any(e["type"] == "Refused" for e in found):
            return None
        name = text.strip(" ?!.")
        if T.is_distinct_product(name) or T.is_radiotracer(name):
            return None             # the list linker above has already applied the rest
        key = T.canonical_key(name)
        exact = [h for h in (self.dic["index"].get(key) or [])
                 if _fold(h["alias"]).strip(" ?!.").lower() == name.lower()]
        if not exact or len({h["drug_id"] for h in exact}) != 1:
            return None
        did = exact[0]["drug_id"]
        return {"type": "Drug", "id": did, "label": self.dic["drugs"][did]["name"],
                "matched_text": name, "matched_alias": exact[0]["alias"]}

    def _link(self, question: str) -> list[dict]:
        found: list[dict] = []
        seen: set = set()
        tokens: list[str] = []
        clause: list[int] = []          # which list piece each token is in
        inner: list[bool] = []          # token inside parentheses
        ci, depth = 0, 0
        for piece in re.split(r"[,;\uff0c\uff1b\u3001]", question):
            for raw in re.split(r"\s+", piece):
                # a slash or bar, glued or free, ends the piece ("bevacizumab - awwb/
                # osimertinib"); it must not hide a product suffix from the guards
                for j, part in enumerate(re.split(r"[/|]", raw)):
                    if j:
                        ci += 1
                    if not part:
                        continue
                    if re.search(r"\w", part):
                        # edge punctuation and wrappers off ("bevacizumab-awwb?",
                        # "(bevacizumab-awwb)"); an isotope ("[89Zr]") is checked as a word
                        t = re.sub(r"^[^\w]+|[^\w]+$", "", part)
                    elif part in ("-", "\u2013", "\u2014", "+", "&"):
                        t = part            # "bevacizumab - awwb" needs its dash
                    else:
                        t = ""
                    if t:
                        tokens.append(t)
                        clause.append(ci)
                        inner.append(depth > 0 or part.startswith("("))
                    depth = max(0, depth + part.count("(") - part.count(")"))
            ci += 1

        # The resolver's refusals apply to questions too: "Mvasi", "liposomal
        # doxorubicin" or "generic version of bevacizumab" must never answer as the
        # originator molecule. The text is cut into list items at commas, semicolons
        # and listing words ("and", "or", "plus", ...). An item that names a distinct
        # product or a tracer anywhere has every drug in it refused: which drug a
        # qualifier describes is not guessed, so "osimertinib with generic version of
        # bevacizumab" refuses both, while "osimertinib and generic version of
        # bevacizumab" keeps osimertinib. Targets and companies in the item still link.
        seg: list[int] = []
        n = 0
        for k, t in enumerate(tokens):
            # "and" inside parentheses describes one product ("doxorubicin
            # (nanoparticle and liposomal formulation)"); it does not list two
            if t.lower() in _JOINERS and not inner[k]:
                n += 1
                seg.append(-1)
                continue
            if k and clause[k] != clause[k - 1]:
                n += 1
            seg.append(n)
        refused: set[int] = {k for k, t in enumerate(tokens)
                             if T.is_distinct_product(t) or T.is_radiotracer(t)}
        drugs = self._drug_spans(tokens, {k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] < 0}, seg)
        dangling = None                 # a qualifier item with no drug, joined by "and"/"or"
        prev_end = -1
        for sid in sorted(set(seg) - {-1}):
            idx = [k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] == sid]
            text = " ".join(tokens[k] for k in idx)
            joined = (dangling is not None and idx[0] > prev_end + 1
                      and all(seg[k] < 0 for k in range(prev_end + 1, idx[0]))
                      and clause[idx[0]] == clause[prev_end])
            prev_end = idx[-1]
            hit = [k for a, b in drugs if seg[a] == sid for k in range(a, b)]
            if _qualified([tokens[k] for k in idx]):
                named = any(k in refused and (
                    T.canonical_key(tokens[k]) in self.dic["index"]
                    or tokens[k].lower() in T.BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | T.REFORMULATION_NAMES)
                    for k in idx)
                if not hit and not named:
                    # "liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin": a bare
                    # qualifier carries over a listing word to the next item's drug; a
                    # product named by itself ("Mvasi and bevacizumab") does not
                    dangling = text
                    continue
                dangling = None
            elif joined and hit:
                text = f"{dangling} ... {text}"
                dangling = None
            else:
                dangling = None if not joined else dangling
                continue
            refused.update(hit)
            found.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": text,
                          "matched_text": text,
                          "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not "
                                 "the originator molecule"})

        matched: set[int] = set()
        for size in range(min(5, len(tokens)), 0, -1):
            for i in range(len(tokens) - size + 1):
                if (refused | matched) & set(range(i, i + size)):
                    continue
                if size > 1 and (len(set(seg[i:i + size])) > 1 or min(seg[i:i + size]) < 0):
                    continue            # a name never spans two list items
                window = " ".join(tokens[i:i + size])
                if len(window) < 3:
                    continue
                key = T.canonical_key(window)

                hits = self.dic["index"].get(key)
                # Several words only make a drug name when the name itself has
                # several words. The key drops spaces, so "is a" read as the
                # abbreviation "isa" (isatuximab) in any question or answer.
                if hits and size > 1 and not re.search(r"[\s\-/]", hits[0]["alias"]):
                    hits = None
                if hits and len({h["drug_id"] for h in hits}) == 1:
                    did = hits[0]["drug_id"]
                    why = self._marked(key, did, tokens, seg, i, i + size)
                    if why:
                        # the name carries a product suffix or sits beside an isotope:
                        # refuse it, never fall back to the bare molecule
                        found.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": why,
                                      "matched_text": why,
                                      "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not "
                                             "the originator molecule"})
                        refused.update(range(i, i + size))
                        continue
                    if ("Drug", did) not in seen:
                        seen.add(("Drug", did))
                        found.append({"type": "Drug", "id": did,
                                      "label": self.dic["drugs"][did]["name"],
                                      "matched_text": window,
                                      "matched_alias": hits[0]["alias"]})
                    matched.update(range(i, i + size))   # words of a longer name are not read again
                    continue

                if window.lower() in self.targets:
                    t = self.targets[window.lower()]
                    if ("Target", t["target_id"]) not in seen:
                        seen.add(("Target", t["target_id"]))
                        found.append({"type": "Target", "id": t["target_id"],
                                      "label": t["symbol"], "matched_text": window})
                    matched.update(range(i, i + size))   # words of a longer name are not read again
                    continue

                if key in self.companies:
                    cid = self.companies[key][0]
                    if ("Company", cid) not in seen:
                        seen.add(("Company", cid))
                        row = self.conn.execute(
                            "SELECT label FROM node WHERE id=?", (cid,)).fetchone()
                        found.append({"type": "Company", "id": cid,
                                      "label": row["label"], "matched_text": window,
                                      "also_matching_ids": self.companies[key][1:]})
                    matched.update(range(i, i + size))   # words of a longer name are not read again
                    continue

                if key in self.disease["index"]:
                    if ("Disease", self.disease["disease_id"]) not in seen:
                        seen.add(("Disease", self.disease["disease_id"]))
                        found.append({"type": "Disease",
                                      "id": self.disease["disease_id"],
                                      "label": self.disease["name"],
                                      "matched_text": window})
        return found

    def _drug_spans(self, tokens: list[str], skip: set[int], seg: list[int]) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
        """Token spans that name exactly one drug, longest first, not overlapping, each
        inside one list item."""
        spans: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        taken: set[int] = set(skip)
        for size in range(min(5, len(tokens)), 0, -1):
            for i in range(len(tokens) - size + 1):
                if taken & set(range(i, i + size)) or len(set(seg[i:i + size])) > 1:
                    continue
                window = " ".join(tokens[i:i + size])
                if len(window) < 3:
                    continue
                hits = self.dic["index"].get(T.canonical_key(window))
                if hits and size > 1 and not re.search(r"[\s\-/]", hits[0]["alias"]):
                    hits = None
                if hits and len({h["drug_id"] for h in hits}) == 1:
                    spans.append((i, i + size))
                    taken.update(range(i, i + size))
        return spans

    def org_family(self, company_id: str) -> list[str]:
        """The organisation plus any companies a curated parent edge links to it."""
        ids = {company_id}
        for row in self.conn.execute(
                "SELECT dst FROM edge WHERE rel='PARENT_OF' AND src=?"
                " AND retired_at IS NULL", (company_id,)):
            ids.add(row["dst"])
        return sorted(ids)

    # -- intent ------------------------------------------------------------ #
    @staticmethod
    def classify(question: str, entities: list[dict]) -> str:
        q = question.lower()
        types = {e["type"] for e in entities}
        if re.search(r"\b(who|which compan|which firm|sponsor|develop|partner)", q):
            return "companies_for_drug" if "Drug" in types else "companies_for_target"
        if re.search(r"\b(trials?|stud(y|ies)|nct)\b", q):
            return "trials_for_entity"
        if re.search(r"\b(target|mechanism|acts? on|moa)\b", q) and "Drug" in types:
            return "targets_of_drug"
        if "Target" in types and re.search(r"\b(drug|inhibitor|agent|candidate)", q):
            return "drugs_for_target"
        if "Company" in types:
            return "company_portfolio"
        if "Drug" in types:
            return "drug_profile"
        if "Target" in types:
            return "drugs_for_target"
        return "unsupported"

    # -- retrieval --------------------------------------------------------- #
    def _rows(self, sql: str, params: tuple) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:
        return list(self.conn.execute(sql, params))

    def retrieve(self, intent: str, entities: list[dict], limit: int = 12):
        ev: list[Evidence] = []
        drug = next((e for e in entities if e["type"] == "Drug"), None)
        target = next((e for e in entities if e["type"] == "Target"), None)
        company = next((e for e in entities if e["type"] == "Company"), None)

        if intent == "companies_for_drug" and drug:
            for r in self._rows("""
                SELECT e.id, e.src, e.confidence, e.props, n.label AS company
                FROM edge e JOIN node n ON n.id = e.src
                WHERE e.rel='SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF' AND e.dst=? AND e.retired_at IS NULL
                ORDER BY json_extract(e.props,'$.evidence_trials') DESC LIMIT ?
            """, (drug["id"], limit)):
                p = json.loads(r["props"])
                ev.append(Evidence(
                    # sponsorship only; the sponsor class is stated so a reader
                    # can tell an originator from a cooperative group
                    statement=f"{r['company']} ({(p.get('sponsor_class') or 'unknown class').lower()} "
                              f"sponsor) is lead sponsor of "
                              f"{p['evidence_trials']} trial(s) evaluating {drug['label']}"
                              " - a sponsorship, not evidence of developing the drug",
                    edge_id=r["id"], relation="SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF", source="derived",
                    source_record=", ".join(p.get("evidence_sample", [])[:3]),
                    fetched_at=None, confidence=r["confidence"],
                    detail={"derived_rule": p.get("rule"),
                            "sponsor_class": p.get("sponsor_class"),
                            "evidence_trials": p["evidence_trials"],
                            "example_trials": p.get("evidence_sample", [])[:3]}))

        elif intent in ("trials_for_entity",) and drug:
            for r in self._rows("""
                SELECT e.id, e.src, e.confidence, e.tier, e.props, e.source_record,
                       e.source_fetched_at, n.props AS tprops, n.label AS nct
                FROM edge e JOIN node n ON n.id = e.src
                WHERE e.rel='EVALUATES' AND e.dst=? AND e.retired_at IS NULL
                ORDER BY e.confidence DESC LIMIT ?
            """, (drug["id"], limit)):
                tp = json.loads(r["tprops"])
                p = json.loads(r["props"])
                ev.append(Evidence(
                    statement=f"{r['nct']} ({', '.join(tp.get('phases') or []) or 'phase n/a'}, "
                              f"{tp.get('status')}) evaluates {drug['label']}",
                    edge_id=r["id"], relation="EVALUATES", source="clinicaltrials.gov",
                    source_record=r["source_record"], fetched_at=r["source_fetched_at"],
                    confidence=r["confidence"], tier=r["tier"],
                    url=TRIAL_URL.format(r["nct"]),
                    detail={"stated_intervention": p.get("stated_intervention"),
                            "title": tp.get("title"), "status": tp.get("status"),
                            "phases": tp.get("phases")}))

        elif intent == "targets_of_drug" and drug:
            for r in self._rows("""
                SELECT e.id, e.confidence, e.props, e.source_record,
                       e.source_fetched_at, n.label AS symbol, n.props AS tprops
                FROM edge e JOIN node n ON n.id = e.dst
                WHERE e.rel='ACTS_ON' AND e.src=? AND e.retired_at IS NULL LIMIT ?
            """, (drug["id"], limit)):
                p = json.loads(r["props"])
                ev.append(Evidence(
                    statement=f"{drug['label']} acts on {r['symbol']}"
                              + (f" ({p['action_type'].lower()})" if p.get("action_type") else ""),
                    edge_id=r["id"], relation="ACTS_ON", source="Open Targets / ChEMBL",
                    source_record=r["source_record"], fetched_at=r["source_fetched_at"],
                    confidence=r["confidence"], detail={"action_type": p.get("action_type")}))

        elif intent == "drugs_for_target" and target:
            for r in self._rows("""
                SELECT e.id, e.confidence, e.props, e.source_record,
                       e.source_fetched_at, n.label AS drug, n.props AS dprops, n.id AS did
                FROM edge e JOIN node n ON n.id = e.src
                WHERE e.rel='ACTS_ON' AND e.dst=? AND e.retired_at IS NULL LIMIT ?
            """, (target["id"], limit)):
                dp = json.loads(r["dprops"])
                trials = self.conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=?"
                    " AND retired_at IS NULL", (r["did"],)).fetchone()[0]
                p = json.loads(r["props"])
                act = (p.get("action_type") or "").lower()
                ev.append(Evidence(
                    statement=f"{r['drug']} acts on {target['label']}"
                              f" ({act + '; ' if act else ''}"
                              f"max stage {dp.get('max_phase_overall') or 'n/a'};"
                              f" {trials} NSCLC trial(s) in this graph)",
                    edge_id=r["id"], relation="ACTS_ON", source="Open Targets / ChEMBL",
                    source_record=r["source_record"], fetched_at=r["source_fetched_at"],
                    confidence=r["confidence"],
                    detail={"drug_id": r["did"], "trials_in_graph": trials,
                            "max_phase_overall": dp.get("max_phase_overall")}))

        elif intent == "company_portfolio" and company:
            # a question about a group means the group and the companies it owns
            org_ids = self.org_family(company["id"]) + company.get("also_matching_ids", [])
            placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(org_ids))
            for r in self._rows(f"""
                SELECT e.id, e.confidence, e.props, n.label AS drug,
                       m.label AS org
                FROM edge e JOIN node n ON n.id = e.dst
                JOIN node m ON m.id = e.src
                WHERE e.rel='SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF' AND e.src IN ({placeholders})
                  AND e.retired_at IS NULL
                ORDER BY json_extract(e.props,'$.evidence_trials') DESC LIMIT ?

exec
"C:\\WINDOWS\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command 'Get-Content audit_evidence_claims/m10/root_checks/run_verification.py -Encoding UTF8; Get-Content audit_evidence_claims/m10/root_checks/run_http.py -Encoding UTF8; Get-Content audit_evidence_claims/m10/root_checks/run_m6_http.py -Encoding UTF8; Get-Content audit_evidence_claims/m10/root_checks/run_extra.py -Encoding UTF8; Get-Content audit_evidence_claims/m10/finalize_integrity.py -Encoding UTF8; Get-Content src/query.py -Encoding UTF8 | Select-Object -First 285' in C:\Users\geoum\Dropbox\GY's Formal Workspace\2026 Job recruting\바이오리서치에이아이\oncokg
 succeeded in 430ms:

import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import time

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]

       "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8", "TMP": str(TMP), "TEMP": str(TMP),
       "TMPDIR": str(TMP)}

def git(*args):
    result = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {"code": result.returncode, "stdout": result.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace"),
            "stderr": result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")}

           "environment": {k: env[k] for k in ("PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE", "PYTHONUTF8", "PYTHONIOENCODING", "TMP", "TEMP", "TMPDIR")},
           "exit_code": run.returncode, "wall_seconds": round(time.perf_counter() - start, 3),
           "hashes_unchanged": before == after, "hashes_after": after}

"""Round 5 HTTP and QA regression checks; all output stays beside this file."""
import ast
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import types
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
PORT = 8897
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
os.environ["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
DB = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"

def git(*args):
    p = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {"code": p.returncode, "stdout": p.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace"),
            "stderr": p.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")}

def post(path, payload):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    try:
        res = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        res = e
    return {"status": res.status, "body": json.loads(res.read())}

before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
initial = {"head": git("rev-parse", "HEAD"), "status": git("status", "--short"),
           "db_sha256": before}
(OUT / "initial_integrity.json").write_text(json.dumps(initial, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
with socket.socket() as s:
    assert s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "audit port already occupied"

log = (OUT / "server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8")
proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)], cwd=ROOT,
                        env=os.environ.copy(), stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
try:
    for _ in range(100):
        if proc.poll() is not None:
            raise RuntimeError("server exited")
        try:
            urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
            break
        except OSError:
            time.sleep(.1)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
    # Re-run every original HTTP case without executing a writer in m1.
    original = (ROOT / "audit_evidence_claims/m1/http_audit.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    adapted = original.replace("8876", str(PORT)).replace("bfcb83d", "514929c")
    (OUT / "http_audit.py").write_text(adapted.replace("OUT.parents[1]", "OUT.parents[2]"), encoding="utf-8")
    run = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(OUT / "http_audit.py")], cwd=ROOT,
                         capture_output=True, env={**os.environ, "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})
    (OUT / "http_stdout.txt").write_bytes(run.stdout + run.stderr)
    assert run.returncode == 0

    regression = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(OUT / "regression_http.py")],
        cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, env={**os.environ, "PYTHONIOENCODING":"utf-8"})
    (OUT / "regression_http_stdout.txt").write_bytes(regression.stdout + regression.stderr)
    assert regression.returncode == 0

    # Load the previous linker/answer module in memory; use the identical database.
    old = types.ModuleType("m2b_old_query")
    old.__file__ = str(ROOT / "src/query.py")
    sys.modules[old.__name__] = old
    code = subprocess.run(["git", "show", "bfcb83d:src/query.py"], cwd=ROOT,
                          capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode("utf-8")
    exec(compile(code, old.__file__, "exec"), old.__dict__)
    baseline = old.OncoKG()
    baseline.conn.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
    html = (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    questions = json.loads(re.search(r"const EX2 = (\[.*?\]);", html, re.S).group(1))
    # Verify CLI/dashboard demos are covered by the page list, without running
    # their entrypoints (which write tracked report/dashboard artifacts).
    sources = {}
    for filename, variable in [("src/query.py", "questions"),
                               ("src/make_dashboard_data.py", "demo_questions")]:
        parsed = ast.parse((ROOT / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assignments = [n for n in ast.walk(parsed) if isinstance(n, ast.Assign)
                       and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == variable for t in n.targets)]
        value = assignments[-1].value
        if isinstance(value, ast.BoolOp):
            value = value.values[-1]
        sources[filename] = ast.literal_eval(value)
        assert set(sources[filename]) <= set(questions)
    answers = []
    for question in questions:
        previous = baseline.answer(question, use_llm=False).to_dict()
        current = post("/api/ask", {"text": question})
        answers.append({"question": question, "http_status": current["status"],
                        "identical_to_bfcb83d": current["body"] == previous,
                        "current": current["body"], "previous": previous})
    baseline.conn.close()
    (OUT / "http_demo_results.json").write_text(json.dumps({"source_lists": sources,
        "answers": answers}, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"demo_questions": len(answers),
        "all_http_200": all(a["http_status"] == 200 for a in answers),
        "all_identical_to_bfcb83d": all(a["identical_to_bfcb83d"] for a in answers)}))
finally:
    proc.terminate()
    proc.wait(timeout=10)
    log.close()
    with socket.socket() as s:
        port_closed = s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
    after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    (OUT / "http_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({"pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT,
        "server_exit": proc.returncode, "port_closed": port_closed,
        "database_unchanged": after == before, "db_sha256_before": before,
        "db_sha256_after": after}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
