"""Verify original tracked bytes and review artifacts; never mutate the application."""
from pathlib import Path
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
import re
import socket
import subprocess

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
before = json.loads((OUT / 'root_checks/integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))

def git(*args):
    got = subprocess.run(['git', *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {'code': got.returncode, 'stdout': got.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace'),
            'stderr': got.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}

changed = []
missing = []
for rel, digest in before['tracked_sha256'].items():
    file = ROOT / rel
    if not file.exists():
        missing.append(rel)
    elif hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
        changed.append(rel)
tracked_now = set(git('ls-files', '-z')['stdout'].split('\0')) - {''}
ports = {}
for port in [8892, 8893, 8896, 8897, 8898, 8899]:
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.settimeout(.2)
        ports[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0
report = (OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
links = re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', report)
broken = [s for s in links if s != 'integrity_final.json' and not (OUT / s.split("#", 1)[0]).exists()]
db_hash = hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
result = {
    'time': datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
    'head_before': before['head'], 'head_after': git('rev-parse', 'HEAD')['stdout'].strip(),
    'tracked_files_hashed': len(before['tracked_sha256']), 'changed_tracked_files': changed,
    'missing_tracked_files': missing, 'new_tracked_paths': sorted(tracked_now-set(before['tracked_sha256'])),
    'tracked_diff': git('diff', '--exit-code'), 'staged_diff': git('diff', '--cached', '--exit-code'),
    'git_status': git('status', '--short'),
    'database_sha256_before': before['db_sha256'], 'database_sha256_after': db_hash,
    'database_unchanged': before['db_sha256'] == db_hash,
    'owned_ports_closed': ports, 'report_links_checked': len(links),
    'broken_report_links': broken,
    'report_final_line': report.rstrip().splitlines()[-1],
    'report_sha256': hashlib.sha256((OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
}
result['passed'] = (not changed and not missing and not result['new_tracked_paths']
    and result['head_before'] == result['head_after']
    and result['tracked_diff']['code'] == result['staged_diff']['code'] == 0
    and result['database_unchanged'] and all(ports.values()) and not broken
    and result['report_final_line'] == 'VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED')
(OUT / 'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
assert result['passed'], 'Final integrity verification failed; inspect JSON'
"""Grounded question answering over the graph.

The retrieval step is the same resolver the pipeline uses, pointed at the
question instead of at a registry record. A question that names "Tagrisso" and a
graph that stores CHEMBL3353410 only meet if one vocabulary serves both, so the
dictionary is reused rather than reimplemented.

Three properties the answering layer holds to:

* **Every statement is traceable.** Each claim carries the edges it rests on,
  each edge carries its source record and fetch time.
* **The graph answers, not the model.** With a key, the language model is given
  the retrieved evidence and returns claims, not prose; each claim is checked
  against the graph and the answer is written from the accepted ones in the
  graph's own words (structured.py). With no key the evidence is rendered
  directly.
* **Nothing unsupported is shipped.** A claim the graph does not support is
  reported and left out. An unverifiable answer is worse than a short one.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import sys
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import config as C
import normalize as N
import textrules as T

DB_PATH = C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite"
TRIAL_URL = "https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/{}"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# evidence
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass
class Evidence:
    statement: str
    edge_id: str
    relation: str
    source: str
    source_record: str | None
    fetched_at: str | None
    confidence: float
    tier: str | None = None
    url: str | None = None
    detail: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def citation(self) -> str:
        base = self.source_record or self.source
        return f"[{base}]"


@dataclass
class Answer:
    question: str
    intent: str
    entities: list[dict]
    text: str
    evidence: list[Evidence]
    generated_by: str
    checks: dict

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "question": self.question,
            "intent": self.intent,
            "entities": self.entities,
            "answer": self.text,
            "generated_by": self.generated_by,
            "checks": self.checks,
            "evidence": [
                {"statement": e.statement, "relation": e.relation, "edge_id": e.edge_id,
                 "source": e.source, "source_record": e.source_record,
                 "fetched_at": e.fetched_at, "confidence": e.confidence,
                 "resolution_tier": e.tier, "url": e.url, **e.detail}
                for e in self.evidence
            ],
        }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# engine
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Words that list separately named things; a list item ends at one. "with" is not among
# them, because it also sits inside product descriptions ("paclitaxel with albumin bound
# nanoparticles").
_JOINERS = {"and", "or", "plus", "versus", "vs", "vs.", "+", "&", "then", "followed",
            "or/and", "and/or"}



# every Unicode dash (category Pd) and minus sign, read as a hyphen
_DASHES = "[" + "".join(re.escape(chr(c)) for c in range(0x110000)
                        if unicodedata.category(chr(c)) == "Pd" or chr(c) in "\u2212\u207b\u208b") + "]"


def _plain(text: str) -> str:
    """Accents off, as the alias key does."""
    t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "")
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFC", "".join(c for c in t if not unicodedata.combining(c)))


def _alnum_words(text: str) -> list[str]:
    """Letters-and-digits words, with every other character a separator."""
    return re.findall(r"[A-Za-z0-9]+", _plain(text))


def _fold(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(_DASHES, "-", unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text or ""))

def _qualified(words: list[str]) -> bool:
    """Does any part of this list item name a distinct product or a tracer?

    The unanchored patterns are tried on the whole item once; the anchored ones (a
    biosimilar suffix, a leading isotope) on every run of up to eight words, so the
    work grows linearly with the item."""
    def hit(text: str) -> bool:
        # the alias key ignores punctuation, underscores and accents, so the guards also
        # see the text with those folded ("bevacizumab-(awwb)", "bevacizumab_awwb",
        # "bevácizumab-awwb")
        plain = _plain(text)
        # and with every separator (punctuation, underscore, spaces) read as one space,
        # so "generic-version of", "albumin - bound" meet the multiword patterns
        return any(T.is_distinct_product(v) or T.is_radiotracer(v)
                   for v in {text, plain, re.sub(r"(?:[^\w\s]|_)+", "-", plain).strip("-"),
                             " ".join(_alnum_words(plain))})

    if hit(" ".join(words)):
        return True
    if any(T.canonical_key(w) in T.BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | T.REFORMULATION_NAMES for w in words):
        return True
    return any(hit(" ".join(words[i:j])) for i in range(len(words))
               for j in range(i + 1, min(len(words), i + 8) + 1))

class OncoKG:
    def __init__(self, db_path: Path = DB_PATH,
                 resolved_path: Path = C.PROCESSED / "resolved.json"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        resolved = json.loads(resolved_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.dic = resolved["dictionary"]
        self.index_keys = list(self.dic["index"].keys())
        self._resolve_cache: dict = {}
        self.disease = resolved["disease"]
        self.targets = {t["symbol"].lower(): t for t in resolved["targets"]
                        if t.get("symbol")}
        # one name can denote several organisations (a group and a subsidiary
        # renamed after it), so a key maps to every matching node, not one.
        self.companies: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        for row in self.conn.execute(
                "SELECT id,label FROM node WHERE type='Company' AND retired_at IS NULL"):
            self.companies.setdefault(T.canonical_key(row["label"]), []).append(row["id"])
            self.companies.setdefault(row["id"].removeprefix("ORG:"), []).append(row["id"])
        # a former name ("BeiGene") must still find the merged node
        for old, same in N.CURATED_SAME_ENTITY.items():
            if same in self.companies:
                self.companies.setdefault(old, []).extend(self.companies[same])
        self.companies = {k: sorted(set(v)) for k, v in self.companies.items()}

    # -- entity linking ---------------------------------------------------- #
    def link_entities(self, question: str) -> list[dict]:
        # one spelling for every check: compatibility forms folded, every dash a hyphen
        question = _fold(question)
        blocked = self._resolver_blocked(question)
        found = self._link(question)
        whole = self._whole_name(question, blocked, found)
        if whole:
            return [whole]
        if not blocked:
            return found
        # a drug the resolver refuses in this text is refused here too, whatever the
        # splitting below made of it (the two must agree before anything is linked)
        out = [e for e in found if not (e["type"] == "Drug" and e["id"] in blocked)]
        for text in sorted({t for t in blocked.values()}):
            if not any(e["type"] == "Refused" and (text in e["label"] or e["label"] in text)
                       for e in out):
                out.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": text, "matched_text": text,
                            "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not the "
                                   "originator molecule"})
        return out

    def _resolver_blocked(self, text: str) -> dict:
        """Drug ids inside the pieces the intervention resolver refuses as a distinct
        product or a tracer, with the refused piece."""
        comps = []
        lone = set()
        pieces = {p.strip() for p in re.split(r"[,;\uff0c\uff1b\u3001/|]", text) if p.strip()}
        for piece in pieces:                         # each distinct list piece once
            words = [w for w in re.split(r"[^\w\-]+", piece) if w]
            if not self._drug_spans(words, set(), [0] * len(words)):
                continue                             # no drug in it: nothing to refuse
            got = self._resolve_cache.get(piece)
            if got is None:
                try:
                    got = N.resolve_drug_string(piece, self.dic, self.index_keys)["components"]
                except Exception:                    # never let a guard crash a lookup
                    got = []
                if len(self._resolve_cache) >= 4096:
                    self._resolve_cache.clear()
                self._resolve_cache[piece] = got
            if len(words) == 1 and T.canonical_key(piece) in self.dic["index"]:
                lone.add(piece)                      # one stored name on its own ("566C")
            comps += got
        out: dict = {}
        accepted = {c.get("drug_id") for c in comps
                    if c.get("drug_id") and not str(c.get("tier", "")).startswith("BLOCKED")}
        for c in comps:
            piece = c.get("component") or ""
            tracer = "radiotracer_guard" in (c.get("rules") or []) or T.is_radiotracer(piece)
            if not (c.get("tier") == "BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT"
                    or (str(c.get("tier", "")).startswith("BLOCKED") and tracer)):
                continue
            if piece in lone:
                continue
            words = [w for w in re.split(r"[\s/|,;]+", piece) if w]
            words = [w.strip(" ?.!:()[]") or w for w in words]
            for a, b in self._drug_spans(words, set(), [0] * len(words)):
                key = T.canonical_key(" ".join(words[a:b]))
                for h in self.dic["index"].get(key, []):
                    # the same molecule named separately and accepted ("biosimilar
                    # anastrozole, Arimidex") is left to the list-item rule below
                    if h["drug_id"] not in accepted:
                        out[h["drug_id"]] = piece
        return out

    def _marked(self, key: str, did: str, tokens, seg, a: int, b: int):
        """The text that makes this drug name a distinct product or a tracer, if any.

        Checked on the same letters-and-digits form the name was looked up by: a known
        biosimilar suffix at the end of the name ("bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab_awwb")
        or as the next word, or an isotope label just before or after it ("[89.Zr]
        trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab")."""
        for sfx in T.BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES:
            stem = key[:-len(sfx)]
            if key.endswith(sfx) and stem and any(
                    h["drug_id"] == did for h in self.dic["index"].get(stem, [])):
                return " ".join(tokens[a:b])
        same = [k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] == seg[a]]
        before = [k for k in same if k < a]
        after = [k for k in same if k >= b]
        # words counted after normalisation, so punctuation never uses up the budget
        prev = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in before))[-2:]
        nxt = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in after))[:2]
        own = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[a:b]))
        lone = not before and not after      # the name is the whole list item
        # an isotope label anywhere in the name ("A33 124i"), or just before or after it
        # ("[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab 89 - Zr"); an isotope-shaped name counts
        # as a name only when it stands alone ("566C", "18f"), never as the substance
        # of a longer phrase ("18F fluorothymidine")
        inside = [own[i:j] for i in range(len(own)) for j in (i + 1, i + 2) if j <= len(own)]
        if lone and T.is_isotope_label(" ".join(own)):
            inside = []                      # the whole item is one isotope-shaped name
        name = " ".join(tokens[a:b])
        if nxt and nxt[0].lower() in T.BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES:
            return f"{name} {nxt[0]}"
        for ws in inside + [prev[-1:], prev, nxt[:1], nxt]:
            if ws and T.is_isotope_label(" ".join(ws)):
                return name if ws in inside else f"{name} ({' '.join(ws)})"
        return None

    def _whole_name(self, text: str, blocked: dict, found: list):
        """The whole input is exactly one stored drug name, letter for letter ("CP-358,774",
        "(E/Z)-AFATINIB"): read it whole rather than as the list its punctuation suggests.

        Only after every refusal has had its say: nothing the resolver or the list
        linker refused, and no product or tracer pattern in the name."""
        if blocked or any(e["type"] == "Refused" for e in found):
            return None
        name = text.strip(" ?!.")
        if T.is_distinct_product(name) or T.is_radiotracer(name):
            return None             # the list linker above has already applied the rest
        key = T.canonical_key(name)
        exact = [h for h in (self.dic["index"].get(key) or [])
                 if _fold(h["alias"]).strip(" ?!.").lower() == name.lower()]
        if not exact or len({h["drug_id"] for h in exact}) != 1:
            return None
        did = exact[0]["drug_id"]

     'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}

       else ['run_http.py','supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py'])

    start=time.perf_counter()
    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/name)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
    rows.append({'script':name,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-start})
    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

## Cost, nonblocking qualifications and demos

All prior cost reproductions complete. Final timing runs were sequential after the other audit workers stopped CPU-intensive execution; unrelated host activity remains uncontrolled. These are observed timings, not latency guarantees or proof that the entire linker is linear. [Reuse manifest](cost/reuse_manifest.json), [execution](cost/execution_isolated.json), [instrumented predicates](cost/perf_current.json), [stress cases](cost/cost_stress.json).

| Workload | Current evidence |
|---|---|
| Original M5 plain 250-word / 499-character linker | Median **0.02209 s**; old cubic scan no longer present |
| Original M7 `";".join(["x"]*2000)`, three-sample same-data comparison | `9bd7cb4` **1.83084 s**, current **0.15246 s** |
| HTTP check of that 3,999-character repeated-piece answer | **0.42500 s**, 200; queued ordinary EGFR map **0.34181 s**, 200 |
| 400 distinct drug-bearing pieces, `";".join(f"isa x{i:04d}" for i in range(400))` | Cold HTTP `/api/check` **2.34826 s**, 200; queued EGFR map **2.27624 s**, 200 |
| Same unique-piece HTTP check, warm cache | **0.54082 s**, 200; ordinary map before/after about **0.036 s** |
| Resolver instrumentation | Unique 400 pieces: **400 cold / 0 warm** calls; repeated drug piece: **1 / 0**; unknown pieces: **0** |
| Natural 4,097th-entry cache rollover | **82/82 fill requests HTTP 200**, 4,096 distinct pieces; first osimertinib and retry both **200 / CHEMBL3353410 / 79 trials** |
| Disease ordinary HTTP request | **2,774 Trials + 300 Targets**, 30 per list; **18,575 bytes / 81.395 ms** |
| Historical 24-request / 12-worker HTTP workload | Expected 200 shapes, **677.505 ms** total |

The repeated-piece and cache defects are **FIXED** for their original reproductions. Distinct legal inputs still hold `engine.lock` long enough to queue ordinary maps for seconds (`src/app.py:247-263`); this remains a performance should-fix, not an asserted new regression or deadlock. The ordinary Disease/concurrency timings overlapped initial audit work. [Historical/current comparison](cost/fresh_cost_comparison.json), [repeated-piece HTTP](cost/fresh_cost_http.json), [unique-piece HTTP](cost/isolated_unique_cost_http.json), [resolver counts](cost/unique_piece_cost.json), [cache HTTP](cost/cache_boundary_http.json), [ordinary HTTP](root_checks/http_results.json).

Other unchanged nonblocking limitations are recorded individually above: unsupported lowercase IDs/investigator labels; raw request parser details in controlled 400 errors; blank submission retaining the old view; Infinity fixtures failing closed; the accepted nonparenthetical list-policy consequence; and prefixed questions containing comma-bearing complete aliases. No new demo regression was established. The specified over-refusals were not used to justify the verdict.

## Standards axis

**Zero documented-standards findings.** The separate standards review examined `git diff bfcb83d...HEAD` application changes and the latest query/tests diff. No tracked AGENTS.md, CONTRIBUTING or coding-standards source exists. The user's evidence-based, audit-only constraints govern; no tracked file was edited. The KOA Studio / KhOpenApi original-versus-modified-code requirement is conditional on code changes, so it is not applicable to this independent no-edit review. [Standards review](db_ui/FINDINGS.md#standards-axis).

## Spec axis

**Four blocking defect families:** M10-B1/B2 are new query regressions; M10-B3/B4 are inherited holes. The original name-resolution guarantee and this round's adjacent-alnum isotope design are not met for the exact inputs above. The 6,469-centre oracle establishes stored-edge/value correctness once a centre is selected; it cannot make a wrong selected molecule correct. No additional feature or implementation change was made. [Independent specification review](attacks/FINDINGS.md#spec-axis-under-400-words).

## Integrity and reproduction

The snapshot has **7 retired nodes, 142 retired edges, zero self-loops and zero live edges with retired endpoints**. All six live node types and all 22 existing relation/direction/type/role combinations were exercised. No new wrong direction, stored value, cut notice or generated-markup injection was established. Snapshot self-loop behavior is code-inspected, not an observed real-row test. [Snapshot](db_ui/db_snapshot_facts.json), [oracle](db_ui/db_validate.json).

`Engine.ask` uses `use_llm=False` (`src/app.py:180-181`); no model was called. Fixtures mutate in-memory DB copies. The application does not globally enforce a read-only SQLite handle, so no-write evidence is the executed paths and before/after hashes rather than a database-permission guarantee. Final HEAD, **2,998 tracked-file SHA-256 comparisons**, empty tracked/staged diffs, unchanged DB, report links and owned-port cleanup are recorded in [integrity_final.json](integrity_final.json). DB SHA-256: **`7341c5a7089731f67356da5a588b269d4b69e8e6c4c054acf19aa4ef2837b529`**.

Run from the repository root; output/temporary paths are confined to m10. Port-sharing scripts must remain sequential. Worker manifests record every reused source and adaptation.

+"""Confine replay outputs to M11 and capture initial tracked-file bytes."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib, json, subprocess
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+(OUT/'tmp').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+tracked = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf8').split('\0')
+hashes = {p:hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in tracked if p}
+initial = dict(head=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),
+               tracked_sha256=hashes, db_sha256=hashes['data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'])
+(OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps(initial,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+manifest=[]
+for src in sorted((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m10/root_checks').glob('*.py')):
+    if src.name == 'build_report.py':
+        continue
+    code=src.read_text(encoding='utf8')
+    # These scripts use __file__-relative outputs; only round-specific input files move.
+    code=code.replace('audit_evidence_claims/m10/', 'audit_evidence_claims/m11/')
+    (OUT/src.name).write_text(code,encoding='utf8')
+    manifest.append(dict(source=src.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),copy=(OUT/src.name).relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),
+                         source_sha256=hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                         adaptations='Outputs relative to copied script; m10 paths remapped to m11; historical baseline reads preserved.'))
+(OUT/'reuse_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(dict(tracked_files=len(hashes),scripts=len(manifest),head=initial['head'])))

SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES = {"free", "only", "like", "base", "type", "form", "dose",
                     "naive", "based", "alone", "mono", "plus", "arm"}

# Since 2017 the FDA gives a four-letter suffix to *originator* biologics as
# well (cemiplimab-rwlc, dostarlimab-gxly, tislelizumab-jsgr), so a suffix on
# its own does not mark a biosimilar. Treating every suffix as one blocked the
# originators. A four-letter lowercase suffix is a biosimilar only when it is a
# known biosimilar suffix; a longer or capitalised suffix is a sponsor name.
BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES = {
    # bevacizumab
    "awwb", "bvzr", "maly", "adcd", "tnjn", "nwgd",
    # trastuzumab
    "dkst", "pkrb", "dttb", "qyyp", "anns", "strf",
    # rituximab
    "abbs", "pvvr", "arrx",
    # pegfilgrastim / filgrastim
    "jmdb", "cbqv", "bmez", "apgf", "fpgk", "pbbk", "sndz", "aafi", "ayow",
    # denosumab
    "bbdz", "bmwo", "bnht", "dssb", "kyqq", "nxxp",
}

# Biosimilar brand names. A brand is not caught by any pattern; these were
# found resolving to their originators (Mvasi, Alymsys, Zirabev -> bevacizumab).
BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS = {
    "mvasi", "zirabev", "alymsys", "vegzelma", "avzivi", "jobevne", "aybintio",
    "onbevzi", "oyavas", "abevmy", "byooviz", "kanjinti", "ogivri", "herzuma",
    "ontruzant", "trazimera", "hercessi", "zercepac", "truxima", "ruxience",
    "riabni", "rixathon", "udenyca", "fulphila", "ziextenzo", "nyvepria",
    "fylnetra", "stimufend", "zarxio", "nivestym", "releuko", "granix",
}


# Reformulated products sold under their own name or code. Checked on the
# words of the string, so a brand in brackets ("Paclitaxel (Abraxane)") is seen.
REFORMULATION_NAMES = {
    "abraxane", "abraxis", "doxil", "caelyx", "lipodox", "onivyde", "depocyt",
    "nanopac", "liporaxel", "lipusu", "genexol", "onureg", "cc486",
    "pazenir", "abi007", "apealea", "dn101", "asentar",
}


def is_distinct_product(text: str) -> bool:
    """True when the string names a product that is not the originator."""
    t = (text or "").strip()
    if not t:
        return False
    if RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT.search(t):
        return True
    words = set(re.findall(r"[a-z]+", t.lower()))
    words |= {canonical_key(w) for w in re.findall(r"[A-Za-z]+[\s\-]?\d+", t)}
    if words & (BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | REFORMULATION_NAMES):
        return True
    m = RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX.match(t)
    if not m:
        return False
    suffix = m.group("suffix")
    if suffix.lower() in SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES:
        return False
    # an FDA suffix is four letters, written in either case ("-rwlc", "-RWLC");
    # a title-case word is a sponsor name ("-Pfizer", "-Teva")
    if len(suffix) == 4 and (suffix.islower() or suffix.isupper()):
        return suffix.lower() in BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES
    return True


# An originator's FDA suffix carries no information about the molecule, so it
# is removed before lookup: "cemiplimab-rwlc" -> "cemiplimab".
RE_FDA_SUFFIX = re.compile(
    r"^(?P<inn>[A-Za-z]{6,}(?:mab|cept|ase|grastim|stim|poetin))\s*-\s*(?P<sfx>[A-Za-z]{4})$")


def strip_fda_suffix(text: str) -> str | None:
    m = RE_FDA_SUFFIX.match((text or "").strip())
    if not m:
        return None
    sfx = m.group("sfx")
    if sfx.lower() in SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES or not (sfx.islower() or sfx.isupper()):
        return None
    return m.group("inn")


# "No pembrolizumab", "without bevacizumab": a negated substance is the
# absence of an exposure. Accepting it as CONTAINED_ALIAS recorded the
# opposite of what the arm says.
RE_NEGATED = re.compile(
    r"^\s*(?:no|not|without|none\s+of|absence\s+of|omit(?:ting|ted)?|"
    r"withdrawal\s+of|discontinu(?:e|ed|ation\s+of))\b[\s\-]*", re.I)


# "pembrolizumab-free regimen", "free of steroids": an absence written after
# the substance rather than before it
# ("free base" is a chemical form, not an absence)
RE_NEGATED_AFTER = re.compile(
    r"\b[A-Za-z0-9]+[\s\-]free\b(?![\s\-]*base)|\bfree\s+of\b", re.I)


def is_negated(text: str) -> bool:
    t = text or ""
    return bool(RE_NEGATED.match(t) or RE_NEGATED_AFTER.search(t))

# A placebo arm must never create a "this company administered this drug" edge.
RE_PLACEBO = re.compile(r"\bplacebo\b|\bsham\b|\bvehicle\s+control\b", re.I)

# Combination separators, longest first so "combined with" wins over "with".
RE_COMBO = re.compile(
    r"\s*(?:\+|&|,|、|，|/(?=\s*[A-Za-z])|\bcombined\s+with\b|"
    r"\bin\s+combination\s+with\b|\bcombination\s+with\b|\bplus\b|\band\b|\bor\b|"
    r"\bfollowed\s+by\b)\s*",
    re.I,
)

RE_PAREN = re.compile(r"\(([^()]*)\)|\[([^\[\]]*)\]")

# Development codes ("AZD9291", "BMS-986504", "PF-07224826", "TSC-204-A0201").
# One character apart is a different molecule, so these must never be matched
# approximately -- the usual string-similarity intuition is wrong here.
RE_DEV_CODE = re.compile(
    r"^[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{2,8}(?:[\s\-][A-Za-z0-9]{1,8})*$")

# Trailing lot/format suffixes that do not change the substance: "BMS-936558-01"
RE_DEV_CODE_SUFFIX = re.compile(r"^(?P<core>[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{3,8})[\s\-]0?\d{1,2}$")
RE_MULTISPACE = re.compile(r"\s+")
RE_PUNCT_EDGE = re.compile(r"^[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+|[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+$")


# Isotope labels on radiotracers: 18F, 68Ga, 99mTc, 177Lu, 64Cu, 89Zr, 131I.
# These look exactly like development codes but name an isotope, not a molecule,
# and the substance they label is usually not in a therapeutic vocabulary at all.
RE_ISOTOPE = re.compile(r"^\[?(\d{1,3}m?)[\s\-]?([A-Za-z]{1,2})\]?$")
ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS = {
    "f", "c", "n", "o", "ga", "cu", "zr", "i", "lu", "tc", "in", "y", "at",
    "ac", "pb", "re", "sm", "sr", "ra", "rb", "br", "h", "p", "s",
}


def is_isotope_label(text: str) -> bool:
    m = RE_ISOTOPE.match((text or "").strip())
    return bool(m and m.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)


# "[18F]F-AraG", "68Ga-DOTATATE": an isotope prefix marks the whole string as a
# radiolabelled tracer, whose unlabelled moiety is not the administered drug.
RE_RADIOTRACER = re.compile(
    r"(?:^|[\s\(\[])\[?\d{1,3}m?[\s\-]?[A-Za-z]{1,2}\]?\s*[-–]\s*\w"
    r"|(?:^|[\s\(])\[\d{1,3}m?[\s\-]?[A-Za-z]{1,2}\]\s*[A-Za-z]", re.I)


def is_radiotracer(text: str) -> bool:
    t = (text or "").strip()
    m = RE_RADIOTRACER.search(t)
    if not m:
        return False
    head = re.match(r"^[\s\(\[]*\[?(\d{1,3})m?[\s\-]?([A-Za-z]{1,2})\]?", t)
    return bool(head and head.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)

"""A local page for trying the pipeline by hand.

    python src/app.py            then open http://localhost:8765

A relation map (a node and the nodes live edges tie it to, a capped list per relation,
click to move on),
then four boxes: type an intervention string the way a registry would write it and see
how the resolver reads it, or ask a question and see the grounded answer with its
evidence, give a structured answer (claims citing evidence edges) and see which
claims the graph supports and the answer written from them, or paste free prose and
see what the auxiliary word-order check flags. All of them call
the functions the pipeline uses (resolve_drug_string, OncoKG.answer,
OncoKG.check_groundedness) on the built artefacts, so what the page shows is what the
pipeline would do. Nothing is written; the language model is never called.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
import sys
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import config as C
import normalize as N
import textrules as T
from query import OncoKG

PAGE = C.WEB / "try.html"
PORT = 8765


class Engine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.kg = OncoKG()
        # A browser opens spare connections it may never use, which would stall a
        # single-threaded server; requests are threaded, the engine is not.
        self.kg.conn.close()
        self.kg.conn = sqlite3.connect(C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite", check_same_thread=False)
        self.kg.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.dic = self.kg.dic
        self.index_keys = self.kg.index_keys
        target_rows, _, _ = N._load("targets")
        self.target_keys = N.target_guard_keys(target_rows, self.dic)

    def resolve(self, text: str) -> dict:
        out = N.resolve_drug_string(text, self.dic, self.index_keys, self.target_keys)
        for c in out["components"]:
            d = self.dic["drugs"].get(c.get("drug_id") or "")
            if d:
                c["drug"] = {k: d.get(k) for k in
                             ("name", "drug_type", "max_phase_overall", "max_phase_in_nsclc")}
                c["drug"]["mechanisms"] = [m.get("mechanismOfAction") if isinstance(m, dict) else m
                                           for m in (d.get("mechanisms") or [])][:3]
                c["drug"]["trials"] = self.kg.conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                    (d["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]
                par = self.dic["drugs"].get(d.get("parent_molecule") or "")
                if par:
                    c["drug"]["parent"] = {"name": par["name"], "trials": self.kg.conn.execute(
                        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                        (par["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]}
            if c.get("candidates"):
                c["candidate_names"] = [
                    (self.dic["drugs"].get(x) or {}).get("name", x) for x in c["candidates"]]
        return out

    def explore(self, text: str, per_group: int = 30) -> dict:
        """One live node and the live nodes its live edges tie it to, grouped by relation.

        `text` is a node id or a name the question linker reads as one entity. A name
        the linker refuses (a biosimilar, a reformulation, a tracer) is reported, never
        mapped to the originator. Each group keeps `per_group` neighbours, sorted as the
        page states: trials by start date (latest first), drugs by trials in the graph,
        sponsors by trials they lead, disease targets by association score. Sponsorship
        keeps its role (lead sponsor or collaborator)."""
        conn = self.kg.conn
        also: list = []
        matched = None
        row = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                           (text,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            nid = row["id"]
        else:
            ents = self.kg.link_entities(text)
            refused = [e for e in ents if e["type"] == "Refused"]
            hits = [e for e in ents if e.get("id") and e["type"] != "Refused"]
            if not hits:
                if refused:
                    return {"refused": refused[0]["label"], "why": refused[0]["why"]}
                return {"not_found": text}
            nid, matched = hits[0]["id"], hits[0].get("matched_alias") or hits[0].get("matched_text")
            also = [{"id": e["id"], "type": e["type"], "label": e["label"]} for e in hits[1:]]
            for oid in hits[0].get("also_matching_ids") or []:   # one alias, several ids
                lab = conn.execute("SELECT type, label FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                                   (oid,)).fetchone()
                if lab and oid != nid:
                    also.append({"id": oid, "type": lab["type"], "label": lab["label"]})
            # the same words may also name an organisation or a target the linker
            # passed over because a drug alias came first ("Roche" is a folic acid alias)
            key = T.canonical_key(text)
            for cid in self.kg.companies.get(key, []):
                if cid != nid and all(x.get("id") != cid for x in also):
                    lab = conn.execute("SELECT label FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                                       (cid,)).fetchone()
                    if lab:
                        also.append({"id": cid, "type": "Company", "label": lab["label"]})
            t = self.kg.targets.get(text.strip().lower())
            if t and t["target_id"] != nid:
                also.append({"id": t["target_id"], "type": "Target", "label": t["symbol"]})
            also += [{"refused": e["label"]} for e in refused]
        node = conn.execute("SELECT id, type, label, props FROM node WHERE id=? "
                            "AND retired_at IS NULL", (nid,)).fetchone()
        if node is None:                              # a name can outlive its node
            return {"not_found": text}
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT e.id AS eid, e.rel, e.src, e.props AS eprops, n.id, n.type, n.label, "
            "n.props AS nprops FROM edge e JOIN node n "
            "ON n.id = CASE WHEN e.src=? THEN e.dst ELSE e.src END "
            "WHERE (e.src=? OR e.dst=?) AND e.retired_at IS NULL AND n.retired_at IS NULL",
            (nid, nid, nid)).fetchall()
        drug_ids = list({r["id"] for r in rows if r["type"] == "Drug"} | {nid})
        trials = {}
        for i in range(0, len(drug_ids), 500):
            chunk = drug_ids[i:i + 500]
            trials.update(conn.execute(               # live trials only
                "SELECT e.dst, COUNT(*) FROM edge e JOIN node t ON t.id = e.src "
                "WHERE e.rel='EVALUATES' AND e.retired_at IS NULL AND t.retired_at IS NULL "
                f"AND e.dst IN ({','.join('?' * len(chunk))}) GROUP BY e.dst", chunk).fetchall())
        groups: dict = {}
        for r in rows:
            ep, np_ = _obj(r["eprops"]), _obj(r["nprops"])
            item = {"id": r["id"], "type": r["type"], "label": r["label"], "edge_id": r["eid"]}
            role = ep.get("role") if r["rel"] == "SPONSORS" else None
            if r["type"] == "Drug":
                item["trials"] = trials.get(r["id"], 0)
                item["max_phase"] = np_.get("max_phase_overall")
            if r["rel"] == "SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF":
                n = ep.get("evidence_trials")
                # a count the edge does not record stays unknown; 0 is only for sorting
                item["evidence_trials"] = n if _number(n) else None
                item["weight"] = n if _number(n) else 0
                item["sponsor_class"] = ep.get("sponsor_class")
            elif r["type"] == "Trial":
                item.update({k: np_.get(k) for k in ("phases", "status", "title", "start_date", "url")})
                item["weight"] = str(np_.get("start_date") or "")
            elif r["type"] == "Drug":
                item["weight"] = item["trials"]
            elif r["rel"] == "ASSOCIATED_WITH":
                score = ep.get("association_score")
                item["score"] = score if _number(score) else None
                item["weight"] = item["score"] or 0
            else:
                item["weight"] = 0
            if r["rel"] == "ACTS_ON":
                item["action"] = ep.get("action_type")
            if role:
                item["role"] = role
            key = (r["rel"], "out" if r["src"] == nid else "in", r["type"], role)
            groups.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
        out = []
        for (rel, direction, ntype, role), items in groups.items():
            items.sort(key=lambda x: x["label"] or "")              # names A to Z
            items.sort(key=lambda x: x["weight"], reverse=True)     # then by the stated weight
            out.append({"rel": rel, "dir": direction, "type": ntype, "role": role,
                        "total": len(items), "items": items[:per_group]})
        out.sort(key=lambda g: -g["total"])
        props = _obj(node["props"])
        return {"center": {"id": node["id"], "type": node["type"], "label": node["label"],
                           "matched_text": matched,
                           "trials": trials.get(node["id"], 0) if node["type"] == "Drug" else None,
                           "props": {k: props.get(k) for k in
                                     ("max_phase_overall", "drug_type", "phases", "status",
                                      "title", "url", "start_date") if props.get(k) is not None}},
                "also": also, "groups": out}

    def ask(self, question: str) -> dict:
        return self.kg.answer(question, use_llm=False).to_dict()

    def structured(self, question: str, payload: str) -> dict:
        """Check a structured answer (claims citing edges) and write the answer from it."""
        kg = self.kg
        entities = kg.link_entities(question)
        evidence = kg.retrieve(kg.classify(question, entities), entities)
        res = kg.verify_structured(payload, evidence)
        res["evidence"] = [{"edge_id": e.edge_id, "statement": e.statement} for e in evidence]
        return res

    def check(self, question: str, answer: str) -> dict:
        """Check an answer someone else wrote against the evidence for the question."""
        kg = self.kg
        entities = kg.link_entities(question)
        intent = kg.classify(question, entities)
        evidence = kg.retrieve(intent, entities)
        checks = kg.check_groundedness(answer, evidence, entities, generated=True,
                                       vocabulary=set(self.dic["index"]),
                                       linker=kg.link_entities)
        return {"intent": intent, "entities": entities, "checks": checks,
                "evidence": [{"statement": e.statement, "source_record": e.source_record,
                              "source": e.source, "url": e.url} for e in evidence[:8]],
                "evidence_count": len(evidence)}


def _number(v) -> bool:
    return isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and v == v


def _obj(raw) -> dict:
    """Stored props as a dict; anything else (null, a list, broken JSON) as empty."""
    try:
        v = json.loads(raw) if raw else {}
    except ValueError:
        return {}
    return v if isinstance(v, dict) else {}


def make_handler(engine: Engine):
    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype: str):
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)

        def do_GET(self):
            if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
                self._send(200, PAGE.read_bytes(), "text/html; charset=utf-8")
            else:
                self._send(404, b"not found", "text/plain")

        def do_POST(self):
            try:
                n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
                body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(min(n, 20000)) or b"{}")
                text = str(body.get("text") or "").strip()
                if len(text) > 500:                      # refused, not silently cut
                    raise ValueError("input is longer than 500 characters")
                if not text:
                    raise ValueError("empty input")
                if self.path not in ("/api/resolve", "/api/ask", "/api/check",
                                     "/api/structured", "/api/explore"):
                    return self._send(404, b"{}", "application/json")
                with engine.lock:
                    if self.path == "/api/explore":
                        res = engine.explore(text)
                    elif self.path == "/api/resolve":
                        res = engine.resolve(text)
                    elif self.path == "/api/ask":
                        res = engine.ask(text)
                    elif self.path == "/api/structured":
                        answer = str(body.get("answer") or "").strip()[:20000]
                        if not answer:
                            raise ValueError("empty answer")
                        res = engine.structured(text, answer)
                    else:
                        answer = str(body.get("answer") or "").strip()[:4000]
                        if not answer:
                            raise ValueError("empty answer")
                        res = engine.check(text, answer)
                self._send(200, json.dumps(res, ensure_ascii=False,
                                           allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8"),
                           "application/json; charset=utf-8")
            except Exception as e:  # show the reason on the page, not a dead request
                self._send(400, json.dumps({"error": str(e)}, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
                           "application/json; charset=utf-8")

        def log_message(self, *a):
            pass
    return Handler


def main() -> None:
    port = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else PORT
    engine = Engine()
    print(f"OncoKG try-it page: http://localhost:{port}", flush=True)
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), make_handler(engine)).serve_forever()
"""Independent CLI HTTP confirmation of candidate round-nine findings."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
PORT = 8899
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
CASES = [
    "trastuzumab 89 - Zr", "trastuzumab [89 - Zr]", "trastuzumab 89 Zr",
    "89 - Zr trastuzumab", "trastuzumab 89-Zr", "trastuzumab",
    "A33 124i", "A33", "124i A33", "A33 124-I", "adakitug",
    "generic-version of bevacizumab", "generic version of bevacizumab", "bevacizumab",
    "albumin - bound paclitaxel", "albumin-bound paclitaxel", "paclitaxel",
    "trastuzumab 89 - Zr, osimertinib", "osimertinib"
]


def post(endpoint, text):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/" + endpoint,
        data=json.dumps({"text": text}).encode(),
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    start = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        response = error
    with response:
        return {"endpoint": endpoint, "input": text, "status": response.status,
                "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
                "response": json.loads(response.read())}

with socket.socket() as sock:
    assert sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "owned port already occupied"
db = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"
before = hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}
with (OUT / "candidate_server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)],
        cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
    results = []
    try:
        for _ in range(100):
            assert proc.poll() is None, "server exited"
            try:
                page = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
                break
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(.1)
        else:
            raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
        for name in CASES:
            for endpoint in ("resolve", "explore"):
                results.append(post(endpoint, name))
            if not name.startswith("CHEMBL"):
                results.append(post("ask", f"Which trials evaluate {name}?"))
        (OUT / "candidate_http.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,
            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print(json.dumps([{"input": r["input"], "status": r["status"],
            "center": r["response"].get("center", {}).get("id"),
            "refused": r["response"].get("refused")}
            for r in results if r["endpoint"] == "explore"], indent=2))
    finally:
        proc.terminate()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        with socket.socket() as sock:
            closed = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
        (OUT / "candidate_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT, "port_closed": closed,
            "database_unchanged": hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == before,
            "requests": len(results), "all_200": all(r["status"] == 200 for r in results),
            "page_matches_worktree": page == (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_bytes()
        }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

# Independent verification — relation map, bfcb83d

Reviewed HEAD: `bfcb83d4320877ffad47083c102ba1c38f691bba`, on 2026-11-03.

**Approval is blocked.** With the supplied database, the page can draw a refused distinct product/tracer as its originator, and can describe a stored trial collaborator as the lead sponsor. These are independently reproducible without changing any application or database file. Retirement fixtures expose two additional inconsistencies. Passing tests do not cover these cases.

## Scope, method, and limitations

- Reviewed the commit's five changed files and the relevant linker/graph-building code. The user brief is the specification; its local copy is [brief.md](brief.md).
- Ran the requested command, `python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider`, with bytecode writing disabled: **317 passed, 1 warning, 8.97 s**. The warning is a `cp949` decoding failure in a subprocess reader thread during `test_correction_check_fails_when_a_correction_is_pending`; it is not a failing assertion. Full output: [pytest.txt](pytest.txt).
- Started the unchanged application with `python -u src/app.py 8876`; exercised `GET /` and `POST /api/explore` over HTTP. The served page is byte-for-byte equal to `web/try.html`. The audit server was stopped afterward, and port 8876 was verified closed. Results and exact inputs: [http_results.json](http_results.json), [http_audit.py](http_audit.py).
- Compared `Engine.explore(id, per_group=100000)` against independently assembled database adjacency for **all 6,469 live nodes**, covering **18 centre/relation/direction/neighbour-type combinations**. This found **zero raw retrieval/value mismatches** in the current snapshot. It verifies stored relationships and values, not the correctness of the Korean wording or the claimed evidence ranking. Evidence: [db_validate.py](db_validate.py), [db_validate.json](db_validate.json).
- Retirement and malformed-property mutations occurred only in isolated in-memory databases. The real database was not changed. Source review, HTTP requests and fixture work called no model.
- Executed the actual page script in a Node VM with minimal DOM stubs and controlled HTTP response completion. Normal map payloads were also compared with actual HTTP results. This reproduces rendering strings, allocation, event handlers and asynchronous state; **it is not a real-browser rendering or exploit test**. Both available browser/native automation paths failed: `The browser access host is unavailable` and native pipe `os error 2`. No screenshot, browser-executed XSS, or browser layout claim is made. Dark mode was out of scope.
- No tracked file was edited. All review-created files are under `audit_evidence_claims/m1/`. An unrelated untracked `audit_evidence_claims/resume1/` directory appeared during review and was not touched. Git and database integrity results are recorded in [integrity.json](integrity.json).

## Standards review

No tracked `AGENTS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING`, or coding-standards document was found. The user-supplied instructions to ground conclusions in evidence and avoid tracked-file edits were followed. No separate coding-standard violation is asserted. The KOA/KhOpenApi code-change comparison instruction does not apply: this audit changes no application code.

## Specification blockers

### B1 — P1: refused products and tracers become originator maps

Exact live-server reproductions:

| POST `/api/explore` JSON | Actual HTTP 200 result | Independent existing resolver result |
|---|---|---|
| `{"text":"bevacizumab - awwb"}` | `BEVACIZUMAB`, `CHEMBL1201583`, 122 trials | `Engine.resolve`: `BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT`; full-string `is_distinct_product` is true |
| `{"text":"89Zr - trastuzumab"}` | `TRASTUZUMAB`, `CHEMBL1201585`, 13 trials | `BLOCKED_CLASS_TERM`; full-string `is_radiotracer` is true |
| `{"text":"[89Zr] trastuzumab"}` | Same trastuzumab originator | Same tracer refusal |

Controls `bevacizumab-awwb` and `[89Zr]trastuzumab` are refused. Thus merely separating punctuation/qualifiers changes a refused product into the originator's relationship map.

**Cause:** `src/query.py:127-132` checks refusal on individual tokens. `src/query.py:146-168` subsequently joins matching windows and accepts aliases without applying the refusal checks to those windows. `src/app.py:81-88` trusts the first accepted hit. This is an inherited linker weakness exposed by the new map, not a claim that the tokenization bug originated in this commit. The map's promised product separation is nevertheless false (`src/app.py:72-74`, `web/try.html:184`).

**Required outcome:** accepted names must satisfy the same product/tracer boundaries as the existing resolver, including spaced and Unicode punctuation variants, before choosing a centre. Do not return an originator map for these exact inputs.

Evidence: [names_product_probe.py](names_product_probe.py), [names_product_bypass.json](names_product_bypass.json). No external pharmacology assumption is needed: the repository's own refusal predicates and resolver identify the prohibited product/tracer.

### B2 — P1: collaborator is described as the trial lead

POST `{"text":"NCT00200395"}`. The returned `SPONSORS|in|Company` group includes Genentech. The page labels that group **“이 임상의 주관 기관”**.

The exact live database edge is:
| S6 action/class specificity | **FIXED** — specific gloss and original AGONIST/BINDING AGENT/class codes. `web/try.html:162-176`. [UI](db_ui/ui_harness_results.json). |
| S7 start date, sponsor-to-Drug global count/phase, zero-trial Drug | **FIXED** — NCT00200395 start 2003-07-02; osimertinib sponsor 14/global 79/APPROVAL; CHEMBL925 count 0. [DB](db_ui/db_assertions.json), [rendered UI](db_ui/ui_harness_results.json). |
| S8 node/edge props null, list, broken JSON | **FIXED** — 12 original requests and restorations return 200. `src/app.py:211-217`. [Fixtures](db_ui/db_fixtures.json). |
| Hostile labels/titles/IDs/URLs; scheme/host/credential/backslash probes | **FIXED for exercised markup paths** — escaping and HTTPS registry allowlist; browser limitation above. `web/try.html:104,180,199,221-235`. [Assertions](db_ui/ui_assertions.json). |
| Unknown type Other / relation NEWREL | **FIXED** — raw fallback, no undefined tooltip. [UI](db_ui/ui_regressions.json). |
| All three / everything documentation overclaims | **FIXED** — All of them / a capped list per relation. `src/app.py:5-11`. |
| Silent 500-character cut: `"x"*500 + " osimertinib"`, `"osimertinib " + "x"*600` | **FIXED** — explicit HTTP 400, no partial map. `src/app.py:239-243`. [HTTP](root_checks/http_results.json). |
| Lowercase IDs / Investigator names: nct00200395, chembl3353410, Armando Santoro Md | **NOT FIXED**, unchanged usability limit. [Controls](names/names_unsupported_forms.json). |
| Raw list/null/malformed JSON/oversized-body errors | **NOT FIXED**, unchanged controlled 400 exposing parse/attribute details; subsequent valid requests succeed. `src/app.py:237-239,267-269`. [HTTP](root_checks/http_results.json). |

+"""Independent small normalization attack matrix, read-only Engine calls."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import sys,json
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'src'))
+from app import Engine
+import query as Q
+import textrules as T
+engine=Engine()
+engine.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+cases=[
+ '[89Z.r] trastuzumab','89Z_r trastuzumab','trastuzumab [89Z.r]',
+ '[89Zr] trastuzumab','89 Zr trastuzumab','[8.9Zr] trastuzumab',
+ 'trastuzumab 8 9 Zr','bevacizumab - a - w - w - b',
+ 'bevacizumab a w w b','bevacizumab - awwb','bevacizumab a.w.w.b',
+ 'trastuzumab labelled with 89Zr','trastuzumab labeled with 89Zr',
+ '89Zr labeled trastuzumab','89Zr conjugated trastuzumab',
+ '99MTc trastuzumab','[99MTc] trastuzumab','99mTc trastuzumab',
+ 'trastuzumab','bevacizumab','osimertinib',
+]
+rows=[]
+for text in cases:
+    folded=Q._fold(text)
+    raw=engine.resolve(text)
+    blocked=engine.kg._resolver_blocked(folded)
+    item=engine.kg._link(folded)
+    whole=engine.kg._whole_name(folded,blocked,item)
+    public=engine.kg.link_entities(text)
+    mapped=engine.explore(text)
+    rows.append(dict(input=text,key=T.canonical_key(text),words=Q._alnum_words(text),
+                     isotope_predicate=T.is_isotope_label(text),raw=raw,blocked=blocked,
+                     item=item,whole=whole,public=public,map=mapped))
+    print(json.dumps(dict(input=text,center=mapped.get('center'),refused=mapped.get('refused')),ensure_ascii=True),flush=True)
+(OUT/'fresh_probe.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+engine.kg.conn.close()

+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+         TMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TEMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TMPDIR=str(OUT/'tmp'))

+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/args[0]),*args[1:]],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(args[0]+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append(dict(args=args,exit_code=p.returncode,seconds=time.perf_counter()-start))
+    (OUT/'execution_remaining.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

+"""Confine replay outputs to M11 and capture initial tracked-file bytes."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib, json, subprocess
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+(OUT/'tmp').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+tracked = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf8').split('\0')
+hashes = {p:hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in tracked if p}
+initial = dict(head=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),
+               tracked_sha256=hashes, db_sha256=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest())
+(OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps(initial,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+manifest=[]
+for src in sorted((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m10/root_checks').glob('*.py')):
+    if src.name == 'build_report.py':
+        continue
+    code=src.read_text(encoding='utf8')
+    # These scripts use __file__-relative outputs; only round-specific input files move.
+    code=code.replace('audit_evidence_claims/m10/', 'audit_evidence_claims/m11/')
+    (OUT/src.name).write_text(code,encoding='utf8')
+    manifest.append(dict(source=src.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),copy=(OUT/src.name).relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),
+                         source_sha256=hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                         adaptations='Outputs relative to copied script; m10 paths remapped to m11; historical baseline reads preserved.'))
+(OUT/'reuse_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(dict(tracked_files=len(hashes),scripts=len(manifest),head=initial['head'])))

+## Blocking findings
+
+### M11-B1 — P1: a separator inside the isotope's element symbol defeats every guard
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"[89Z.r] trastuzumab"}`.
+
+**Actual:** HTTP 200, centre **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, **13 trials**, matched stored alias **`89zr-trastuzumab`**, no Refused entity or refusal notice. Trial and sponsor QA each return **12 originator evidence rows**. `89Z_r trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab [89Z.r]` also draw the same originator. Adding `, osimertinib` still selects the forbidden trastuzumab centre. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/new_leaks_http.json), [stages and exact predecessor comparison](attacks/key_equivalence_results.json).
+
+| Stage | Result for `[89Z.r] trastuzumab` |
+|---|---|
+| Raw resolver | EXACT → CHEMBL1201585, `extract_parenthetical`; no blocked component |
+| Resolver veto | `{}` |
+| Item linker / public linker | Drug TRASTUZUMAB; no Refused |
+| Whole-name shortcut | `None`; it does not override a refusal |
+| Map | Originator and its stored live relationships |
+
+**Evidence for equivalence:** `canonical_key('[89Z.r] trastuzumab')` equals the stored `89zr-trastuzumab` key. The positive matcher consumes that alias. The controls `[89Zr] trastuzumab`, `[89.Zr] trastuzumab` and `89 Zr trastuzumab` refuse. This is the same dictionary-recognized tracer alias with punctuation inside the element symbol, not an invented drug identity.
+
+**Cause:** `_alnum_words` makes `['89Z', 'r']` (`src/query.py:110-112`). `_marked` passes their space-joined forms to `is_isotope_label` (`src/query.py:249-265`), whose regex permits separation between the mass and element, but not inside `Zr` (`src/textrules.py:289-298`). Neither `89Z` nor `89Z r` passes; `Z` alone is not in the element set. `_qualified`'s normalization variants also miss (`src/query.py:125-141`), while positive lookup erases the separator and matches `89zrtrastuzumab` (`src/query.py:383-405`). The final map trusts the surviving positive hit (`src/app.py:87-95`).
+
+**Required outcome:** the refusal decision must retain isotope identity across punctuation accepted by positive alias lookup, both inside a consumed alias and beside a bare drug. Preserve the existing lone-name controls. Immediate-predecessor query code also leaks this input against the same current app/rules/database; this is a query-module counterfactual, not a reconstructed old deployment. [Independent adjudication](db_ui/attack_adjudication.md).
+
+### M11-B2 — P1: a punctuation-only token pushes a known biosimilar suffix beyond the positive window
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"bevacizumab a - w w b"}`.
+
+**Actual:** HTTP 200, centre **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, no refusal. Trial and sponsor QA each return **12 originator evidence rows**. `bevacizumab - a - w - w - b` and underscore-separated variants also leak. Appending `, osimertinib` still draws bevacizumab. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/new_leaks_http.json), [current/predecessor stages](attacks/key_equivalence_results.json).
+
+| Stage | Result for `bevacizumab a - w w b` |
+|---|---|
+| Raw resolver | ALIAS → CHEMBL1201583; no blocked component |
+| Resolver veto | `{}` |
+| Item linker / public linker | Bare BEVACIZUMAB; no Refused |
+| Whole-name shortcut | `None` |
+| Map | Originator with its stored live relationships |
+
+**Evidence for equivalence:** the entire input has the same canonical key and stored alias hits as `bevacizumab-awwb`. The controls `bevacizumab-awwb`, `bevacizumab a.w.w.b` and **`bevacizumab a w w b`** refuse. Adding one standalone hyphen to the last refused input makes it pass.
+
+**Cause:** the extra hyphen makes six raw tokens. Both drug-span detection and positive windows are limited to five (`src/query.py:374-384,446-461`), so the full suffix-bearing name is no longer matched and `_marked` receives only bare `bevacizumab`. Its same-name suffix check sees no suffix, and its next-word check sees only `a` (`src/query.py:241-265`). `_qualified` does see the whole item, including normalized `bevacizumab a w w b`, but no guard reassembles the suffix letters into the canonical `awwb` used by positive lookup (`src/query.py:125-141`). This is not a claim that the eight-word scan misses the six-token input: it scans it and still returns false.
+
+**Required outcome:** punctuation-only tokens must not turn a recognized, refused suffix-bearing product into an accepted bare parent. Check normalized qualifier identity independently of whether the full positive window fits. Immediate-predecessor query code also leaks; do not attribute this hole to the newest commit. [Independent adjudication](db_ui/attack_adjudication.md).

+"""Independent small normalization attack matrix, read-only Engine calls."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import sys,json
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'src'))
+from app import Engine
+import query as Q
+import textrules as T
+engine=Engine()
+engine.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+cases=[
+ '[89Z.r] trastuzumab','89Z_r trastuzumab','trastuzumab [89Z.r]',
+ '[89Zr] trastuzumab','89 Zr trastuzumab','[8.9Zr] trastuzumab',
+ 'trastuzumab 8 9 Zr','bevacizumab - a - w - w - b',
+ 'bevacizumab a w w b','bevacizumab - awwb','bevacizumab a.w.w.b',
+ 'trastuzumab labelled with 89Zr','trastuzumab labeled with 89Zr',
+ '89Zr labeled trastuzumab','89Zr conjugated trastuzumab',
+ '99MTc trastuzumab','[99MTc] trastuzumab','99mTc trastuzumab',
+ 'trastuzumab','bevacizumab','osimertinib',
+]
+rows=[]
+for text in cases:
+    folded=Q._fold(text)
+    raw=engine.resolve(text)
+    blocked=engine.kg._resolver_blocked(folded)
+    item=engine.kg._link(folded)
+    whole=engine.kg._whole_name(folded,blocked,item)
+    public=engine.kg.link_entities(text)
+    mapped=engine.explore(text)
+    rows.append(dict(input=text,key=T.canonical_key(text),words=Q._alnum_words(text),
+                     isotope_predicate=T.is_isotope_label(text),raw=raw,blocked=blocked,
+                     item=item,whole=whole,public=public,map=mapped))
+    print(json.dumps(dict(input=text,center=mapped.get('center'),refused=mapped.get('refused')),ensure_ascii=True),flush=True)
+(OUT/'fresh_probe.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+engine.kg.conn.close()

+"""Sequential inherited CLI/HTTP replays including exact M10 reproductions."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import os, subprocess, sys, time, json
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+         TMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TEMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TMPDIR=str(OUT/'tmp'))
+rows=[]
+for args in [['run_m6_http.py','m6_http'],['run_extra.py'],['candidate_confirm.py'],['isotope_confirm.py']]:
+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/args[0]),*args[1:]],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(args[0]+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append(dict(args=args,exit_code=p.returncode,seconds=time.perf_counter()-start))
+    (OUT/'execution_remaining.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

+"""Confine replay outputs to M11 and capture initial tracked-file bytes."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib, json, subprocess
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+(OUT/'tmp').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+tracked = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf8').split('\0')
+hashes = {p:hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in tracked if p}
+initial = dict(head=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),
+               tracked_sha256=hashes, db_sha256=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest())
+(OUT/'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps(initial,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+manifest=[]
+for src in sorted((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m10/root_checks').glob('*.py')):
+    if src.name == 'build_report.py':
+        continue
+    code=src.read_text(encoding='utf8')
+    # These scripts use __file__-relative outputs; only round-specific input files move.
+    code=code.replace('audit_evidence_claims/m10/', 'audit_evidence_claims/m11/')
+    (OUT/src.name).write_text(code,encoding='utf8')
+    manifest.append(dict(source=src.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),copy=(OUT/src.name).relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),
+                         source_sha256=hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                         adaptations='Outputs relative to copied script; m10 paths remapped to m11; historical baseline reads preserved.'))
+(OUT/'reuse_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(dict(tracked_files=len(hashes),scripts=len(manifest),head=initial['head'])))

## Demo and broader lookup effects

All seven original page/CLI QA demos retain evidence counts **12, 12, 11, 1, 12, 0, 0**. Six complete answers equal bfcb83d; Mvasi differs only in refusal metadata. [Demo comparison](names_demo_results.json).

The newly refused bare-alias set has 37 more entries than m10. It includes intended labelled-product refusals such as A33 124i and numerous true isotope-bearing stored substances; it is not 37 defects. [Exact loss set](new_alias_losses.json). Apparent nonblocking over-refusals include `9-amino-20(s)-camptothecin` (20(s) stereochemical notation read as an isotope), `il-6ra` (TOCILIZUMAB alias contains 6ra), and `TAK-659F` (MIVAVOTINIB development alias contains 659F). No wrong molecule is drawn. The supplied M5-S2 and M9-S1 accepted over-refusals remain recorded above and are not reasons for blocking approval.

No application code changed; the conditional KOA/KhOpenApi original-versus-modified code requirement does not apply. Outputs reside under m11 and stage probes reopen database handles read-only. Root owns complete tracked-file and DB hash verification.

Set PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1, PYTHONUTF8=1 and TMP/TEMP/TMPDIR under m11/names/tmp. Port-sharing scripts must be sequential. Counterfactuals replace only their stated query methods/module and retain current app/rules/data; they are not historical deployment reconstructions.
# Independent adjudication of the two normalization findings

Reviewed current source and the recorded current/previous stage traces in `../root_checks/fresh_probe.json` and `../attacks/key_equivalence_results.json`. **Both are approval blockers under the supplied refusal guarantee.** They are inherited bypasses (the recorded `c364c84` comparison has the same outcomes), not demonstrated regressions introduced by `2b9fc92`.

| Attack and refused control | Recorded evidence | Judgment |
|---|---|---|
| `[89Z.r] trastuzumab` versus `[89Zr] trastuzumab` | Both lookup keys are `89zrtrastuzumab`, with the same stored `89zr-trastuzumab` alias pointing to `CHEMBL1201585`. Attack: raw resolver **EXACT** to TRASTUZUMAB after `extract_parenthetical`; `_qualified=False`, blocked `{}`, item/public linker **Drug**, whole-name shortcut `None`; map draws TRASTUZUMAB with 13 trials. Control: raw `radiotracer_guard`, public **Refused**, no map centre. | **Blocker.** The product spelling changes only by an inserted period inside the recognized isotope symbol. The matcher explicitly resolves it through the same stored tracer alias while refusal checks fail to recognize that same content. |
| `bevacizumab a - w w b` versus `bevacizumab a w w b` | Both keys are `bevacizumabawwb`; both alphanumeric word arrays are `[bevacizumab,a,w,w,b]`; both have the same stored `Bevacizumab awwb` hit (`CHEMBL1201583`). Attack: raw resolver **ALIAS**, `_qualified=False`, blocked `{}`, item/public linker **Drug**, whole-name shortcut `None`; map draws BEVACIZUMAB with 122 trials. Control: item/public **Refused**, no centre. | **Blocker.** Adding one punctuation-only token defeats a suffix refusal already applied to an otherwise identical normalized input. |

Neither attack's raw resolver result is UNRESOLVED: the isotope attack returns EXACT and the suffix attack returns ALIAS. No stage records a refusal for either attack. The first case's whole-name shortcut does not overturn a refusal; it returns `None`. Recorded trial/sponsor QA also returns 12/12 evidence rows for each attack, versus 0/0 for each control.

## Causes

- **Isotope:** `canonical_key` deletes non-alphanumeric characters (`src/textrules.py:339-346`), but `_alnum_words` turns them into word boundaries (`src/query.py:111-113`). `_marked` then tests one- or two-word windows joined by a space (`src/query.py:250-265`). `89Z.r` becomes `89Z r`: `89Z` uses unrecognized element `Z`, while `89Z r` has a separator inside the element symbol, which `RE_ISOTOPE` does not accept (`src/textrules.py:289-298`). The alias key still recognizes `89zr-trastuzumab`. Raw bracket removal and exact parent lookup independently bypass refusal (`src/textrules.py:359-363`; `src/normalize.py:738-758`).
- **Suffix:** a standalone hyphen remains a raw token (`src/query.py:309-310`). Both positive lookup and `_drug_spans` cap windows at five raw tokens (`src/query.py:378-389,446-463`). The control's five-token alias reaches `_marked` and its canonical suffix check (`src/query.py:241-245`); the six-token attack cannot. Bare `bevacizumab` is then matched, and the adjacent check examines only the first normalized word `a`, not the suffix assembled from `a w w b` (`src/query.py:250-263`). The item guard's separator-to-space view still leaves four suffix letters separated, so its anchored suffix expression does not fire (`src/query.py:125-141`; `src/textrules.py:156-157,202-223`).

The evidence does not require an external pharmacological claim. `awwb` and `zr` are explicit project guard vocabulary (`src/textrules.py:169-171,290-298`), the exact controls are refused, and both attacks preserve the letters/digits and the stored lookup identity. Canonical-key equality alone would not justify arbitrary synonym equivalence; here it corroborates these specific punctuation/tokenization changes and the already recorded refusal semantics. No speculative language variants were considered.

'ﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺﷺ
